```bash

```

Read in parallel (HANDOFF.md uses **read-from-end strategy** — SSP-01 v6.5.0; arquivo monolítico newest-first em ~3170+ linhas, leitura completa estoura limite de 25k tokens):
- `HANDOFF.md` with `limit=71` → Task State header + FIRST ACTION FOR NEXT AGENT (sempre estável, contém summary da fase atual selada)
- `HANDOFF.md` with `offset=<total_lines - 400>` `limit=400` → últimas 1-2 Records + Pending Actions + GraphRAG State + Infrastructure + Key Files (suffix sections always at the tail)
- `CLAUDE.md` (architecture rules, data flow, key files — incluindo MCORCH Master Execution Protocol + Survival Laws link)
- `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` (sprint goal, 4Cs snapshot, top gaps)
- `/home/ubuntu/.claude/projects/-home-gcrUX-htdocs-constellation-orchestra/memory/MEMORY.md` (memory index)

> ⚠️ **Auditoria histórica de seals antigos:** usar `Read` com offset arbitrário em HANDOFF.md (fora do fluxo padrão de pickup). Ordering newest-first: seal mais antigo ≈ linha 2805, seal mais recente logo após linha 71. Cada `## <Phase> Record (YYYY-MM-DD)` marca um seal. Manter o Read em chunks ≤ 400 linhas para preservar budget de tokens.

Also check for loose files in scratch/:
```bash
ls scratch/ 2>/dev/null && echo "⚠️ scratch/ has files — consider moving to .claude/scripts/db/" || echo "scratch/ clean ✅"
```

### BoK Gate check (v3 — MCORCH Master Execution Protocol)

For each BoK suite in `docs/bok/<slug>/`, verify completeness:
```bash
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete" || echo "⚠️ $slug — missing: ${missing[*]}"
done
```

If the user mentions working on a **new module / complex feature / integration / architectural change**:
- Check whether `docs/bok/<expected-slug>/` exists with all 9 docs.
- If missing: surface in the **ALERTAS** section as a critical block per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.
- Recommend `/bok-scribe <idea summary>` before any code is written.

## Rules

- **TypeScript errors → report immediately.** If there are TS errors, list them under ALERTAS. Do not start implementing new features until errors are resolved (unless the user explicitly asks to proceed despite errors).
- **Unhealthy containers → report with triage.** Suggest the fix command if known (e.g., `docker compose restart mcorch-vector-engine` for chroma).
- **Uncommitted changes → describe, do not commit.** Only describe what exists in the working tree; committing is the user's call.
- **Keep the brief tight.** No padding. No repetition of CLAUDE.md architecture. The user already knows the architecture — they need the delta: what changed, what's broken, what's next.
- **Pending Actions priority order:** CRITICAL (before first client) > NON-CRITICAL > Phase Zeta > Core v3 > Epsilon v9.
- **Knowledge Mesh numbers:** use `GraphRAG State` section from HANDOFF.md as the source. Do not invent counts.

> 3318	### Embedding Model

> 3321	
> 3322	### Embedding Pipeline (Autônomo — 2026-05-03)
> 3323	Três camadas eliminam intervenção manual:
> 3324	1. **INSERT trigger** — `trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert` (pg_net) dispara `embed-mcorch-node` assincronamente em todo INSERT onde `embedding IS NULL`. Nunca bloqueia o INSERT.
> 3325	2. **Post-commit hook** — `.git/hooks/post-commit` roda `node scripts/ingest-codebase.ts --quiet` em background após cada commit → re-ingesta AST.
> 3326	3. **MeshControlPanel** — UI bottom-left do MatrixPage: "Sync Embeddings" (`trigger-embeddings`) e "Refresh Mesh Stats" (`trigger-ingest`).
> 3327	
> 3328	### Comandos de manutenção da Mesh
> 3329	```bash
> 3330	# Verificar nós sem embedding (deveria retornar array vazio):
> 3331	curl -s "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?embedding=is.null&select=id,name" \
> 3332	  -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" | python3 -c "import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(f'{len(d)} nós sem embedding')"
> 3333	
> 3334	# Backfill manual (Bun-native — requer .env):
> 3335	bun run scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts
> 3336	
> 3337	# Backfill de código-fonte nos nós AST:
> 3338	bun run scripts/backfill-node-content.ts
> 3339	
> 3340	# Re-sync AST completo:
> 3341	cd packages/gitnexus-core && npm run sync-mesh
> 3342	
> 3343	# Acompanhar re-ingesta pós-commit:
> 3344	tail -f /tmp/mcorch-ingest.log
> 3345	```

> 58	## Higgsfield — opções avançadas de vídeo (núcleo do pedido)
> 59	
> 60	Base do port: `ImageToVideoInspector` do viralmind (model · duração · aspect ratio · motion strength · imagem-fonte do upstream). **Estender** com o conjunto avançado:
> 61	
> 62	- **Modelo de vídeo:** DoP (`/higgsfield-ai/dop/standard` — "Director of Photography", controle de câmera) × Kling (`/kling-video/v2.1/pro/image-to-video` — qualidade premium). Cards com tier + `higgsfieldCost` + eta.
> 63	- **Duração:** 5 / 10 / 15s (custo Higgsfield escala com duração).
> 64	- **Aspect ratio:** 9:16 · 16:9 · 1:1 · 4:5.
> 65	- **Parâmetros DoP avançados:** presets de movimento de câmera, intensidade de movimento, resolução (720p/1080p), seed, enhance-prompt — **nomes exatos confirmados contra a doc da API Higgsfield na implementação** (o cliente viralmind expõe o básico; a API DoP suporta mais).
> 66	- **Higgsfield Soul (imagem):** disponível como modelo no nó GenerateImage — `aspect_ratio` + `resolution` (720p/1080p).
> 67	- **Custo real:** cada modelo/duração tem `higgsfieldCost` (créditos Higgsfield) no catálogo, exibido junto do `coinCost`. Investigar endpoint de saldo Higgsfield para mostrar "X/30 créditos restantes"; se não houver, exibir só o custo estimado por operação.

> 14	 * RLS-aligned: filter `user_id=eq.<auth.uid>` ensures cross-tenant isolation at the channel level.
> 15	 */
> 16	export function useCanvasJobsRealtime(projectId?: string): void {
> 17	  const qc = useQueryClient();
> 18	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 19	
> 20	  useEffect(() => {
> 21	    if (!user) return;
> 22	
> 23	    const channel = supabase
> 24	      .channel(`canvas_jobs_${user.id}`)
> 25	      .on(
> 26	        'postgres_changes',
> 27	        {
> 28	          event: 'UPDATE',
> 29	          schema: 'public',
> 30	          table: 'vm_canvas_executions',
> 31	          filter: `user_id=eq.${user.id}`,
> 32	        },
> 33	        (payload) => {
> 34	          const row = payload.new as { id: string; project_id: string; status: string };
> 35	          qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-execution', row.id] });
> 36	          if (projectId && row.project_id === projectId) {
> 37	            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-assets', projectId] });
> 38	            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-executions', projectId] });
> 39	          }
> 40	        },
> 41	      )
> 42	      .subscribe();
> 43	
> 44	    return () => {
> 45	      supabase.removeChannel(channel);
> 46	    };
> 47	  }, [user?.id, projectId, qc]);
> 48	}
> 49	
> ```

> 334	async function generateOpenAI(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 335	  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("OpenAI API key not configured");
> 336	
> 337	  const model = body.model || "dall-e-3";
> 338	  const size = body.parameters?.size || "1024x1024";
> 339	
> 340	  const resp = await fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/images/generations", {
> 341	    method: "POST",
> 342	    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 343	    body: JSON.stringify({
> 344	      model,
> 345	      prompt: body.prompt,
> 346	      n: 1,
> 347	      size,
> 348	      response_format: "b64_json",
> 349	      ...(body.parameters?.quality ? { quality: body.parameters.quality } : {}),
> 350	      ...(body.parameters?.style ? { style: body.parameters.style } : {}),
> 351	    }),
> 352	  });
> 353	
> 354	  if (!resp.ok) {
> 355	    const t = await resp.text();
> 356	    throw new Error(`OpenAI error ${resp.status}: ${t}`);
> 357	  }
> 358	
> 359	  const data = await resp.json();
> 360	  return { url: data.data?.[0]?.url || "", b64: data.data?.[0]?.b64_json || "" };
> 361	}
> 362	
> 363	async function generateReplicate(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 364	  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("Replicate API key not configured");
> 365	
> 366	  const modelMap: Record<string, string> = {
> 367	    "flux-1.1-pro": "black-forest-labs/flux-1.1-pro",
> 368	    "flux-schnell": "black-forest-labs/flux-schnell",
> 369	    "sdxl": "stability-ai/sdxl:39ed52f2a78e934b3ba6e2a89f5b1c712de7dfea535525255b1aa35c5565e08b",
> 370	  };
> 371	
> 372	  const modelId = modelMap[body.model] || body.model;
> 373	  const input: Record<string, any> = {
> 374	    prompt: body.prompt,
> 375	    ...(body.negative_prompt ? { negative_prompt: body.negative_prompt } : {}),
> 376	    ...(body.parameters?.width ? { width: body.parameters.width } : {}),
> 377	    ...(body.parameters?.height ? { height: body.parameters.height } : {}),
> 378	    ...(body.parameters?.num_inference_steps ? { num_inference_steps: body.parameters.num_inference_steps } : {}),
> 379	    ...(body.parameters?.guidance_scale ? { guidance_scale: body.parameters.guidance_scale } : {}),
> 380	  };
> 381	
> 382	  // Create prediction
> 383	  const createResp = await fetch("https://api.replicate.com/v1/predictions", {
> 384	    method: "POST",
> 385	    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 386	    body: JSON.stringify({ model: modelId.includes(":") ? undefined : modelId, version: modelId.includes(":") ? modelId.split(":")[1] : undefined, input }),
> 387	  });
> 388	
> 389	  if (!createResp.ok) {
> 390	    const t = await createResp.text();
> 391	    throw new Error(`Replicate error ${createResp.status}: ${t}`);
> 392	  }
> 393	
> 394	  let prediction = await createResp.json();
> 395	
> 396	  // Poll for completion (max 120s)
> 397	  const maxWait = 120_000;
> 398	  const start = Date.now();
> 399	  while (prediction.status !== "succeeded" && prediction.status !== "failed" && Date.now() - start < maxWait) {
> 400	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));
> 401	    const pollResp = await fetch(prediction.urls.get, {
> 402	      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}` },
> 403	    });
> 404	    prediction = await pollResp.json();
> 405	  }
> 406	
> 407	  if (prediction.status === "failed") {
> 408	    throw new Error(`Replicate failed: ${prediction.error}`);
> 409	  }
> 410	
> 411	  const output = Array.isArray(prediction.output) ? prediction.output[0] : prediction.output;
> 412	  return { url: output || "" };
> 413	}
> 414	
> 415	async function generateOpenRouter(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 416	  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("OpenRouter API key not configured");
> 417	
> 418	  const resp = await fetch("https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/images/generations", {
> 419	    method: "POST",
> 420	    headers: {
> 421	      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
> 422	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
> 423	      "HTTP-Referer": "https://mcorch.com",
> 424	      "X-Title": "GCRUX Canvas Studio",
> 425	    },
> 426	    body: JSON.stringify({
> 427	      model: body.model || "dall-e-3",
> 428	      prompt: body.prompt,
> 429	      n: 1,
> 430	      size: body.parameters?.size || "1024x1024",
> 431	      response_format: "b64_json",
> 432	    }),
> 433	  });
> 434	
> 435	  if (!resp.ok) {
> 436	    const t = await resp.text();
> 437	    throw new Error(`OpenRouter error ${resp.status}: ${t}`);
> 438	  }
> 439	
> 440	  const data = await resp.json();
> 441	  return { url: data.data?.[0]?.url || "", b64: data.data?.[0]?.b64_json || "" };

> 444	async function generateHiggsfield(apiKey: string, apiSecret: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {
> 445	  if (!apiKey || !apiSecret) {
> 446	    throw new Error("Higgsfield credentials not configured (HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY / HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY_SECRET)");
> 447	  }
> 448	
> 449	  // Correct Higgsfield Platform API — base + auth + routes per the tested
> 450	  // viralmind integration (src/lib/higgsfield.server.ts). The previous
> 451	  // api.higgsfield.ai/v1/generations endpoint was wrong and never worked.
> 452	  const BASE_URL = "https://platform.higgsfield.ai";
> 453	  const authHeader = `Key ${apiKey}:${apiSecret}`;
> 454	  const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 3000;
> 455	  const MAX_POLL_MS = 90_000;
> 456	
> 457	  // Derive Soul aspect ratio from incoming size / aspect_ratio params.
> 458	  // Canonical Higgsfield ARs (per /docs/guides/images): 16:9 · 9:16 · 1:1 · 4:5.
> 459	  const SIZE_TO_AR: Record<string, string> = {
> 460	    "1024x1024": "1:1",
> 461	    "1792x1024": "16:9",
> 462	    "1024x1792": "9:16",
> 463	    "1024x1280": "4:5",
> 464	    "1280x1024": "5:4",
> 465	  };
> 466	  const VALID_AR = new Set(["16:9", "9:16", "1:1", "4:5"]);
> 467	  const sizeKey = body.parameters?.size ? String(body.parameters.size) : "";
> 468	  const requestedAr = body.parameters?.aspect_ratio || SIZE_TO_AR[sizeKey] || "1:1";
> 469	  const aspectRatio = VALID_AR.has(requestedAr) ? requestedAr : "1:1";
> 470	  const resolution = body.parameters?.resolution === "720p" ? "720p" : "1080p";
> 471	
> 472	  // NOTE (Phase 5.3 — TODO): video routes (DoP/Kling/Seedance) will be added
> 473	  // here using `?hf_webhook=URL` query param (NOT body) per validation doc
> 474	  // .claude/context/higgsfield-api-validation-2026-05-15.md § Webhook spec.
> 475	  // Video gens exceed the current 90s poll window — webhook is mandatory.
> 476	
> 477	  // ── Submit Soul text-to-image job ──────────────────────────────────
> 478	  const submitResp = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/higgsfield-ai/soul/standard`, {
> 479	    method: "POST",
> 480	    headers: {
> 481	      Authorization: authHeader,
> 482	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
> 483	      Accept: "application/json",
> 484	    },
> 485	    body: JSON.stringify({ prompt: body.prompt, aspect_ratio: aspectRatio, resolution }),
> 486	  });
> 487	
> 488	  if (!submitResp.ok) {
> 489	    const t = await submitResp.text();
> 490	    if (submitResp.status === 401 || submitResp.status === 403) {
> 491	      throw new Error("Higgsfield: credenciais inválidas");
> 492	    }
> 493	    if (submitResp.status === 402) {
> 494	      throw new Error("Higgsfield: saldo de créditos esgotado");
> 495	    }
> 496	    if (submitResp.status === 429) {
> 497	      throw new Error("Higgsfield: limite de requisições atingido");
> 498	    }
> 499	    throw new Error(`Higgsfield submit error ${submitResp.status}: ${t.slice(0, 200)}`);
> 500	  }
> 501	
> 502	  const submitData = await submitResp.json();
> 503	  const requestId = submitData.request_id || submitData.id;
> 504	  if (!requestId) throw new Error("Higgsfield: resposta de submit inválida (sem request_id)");
> 505	
> 506	  // ── Poll until terminal state ──────────────────────────────────────
> 507	  const started = Date.now();
> 508	  while (Date.now() - started < MAX_POLL_MS) {
> 509	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, POLL_INTERVAL_MS));
> 510	    const statusResp = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}/requests/${requestId}/status`, {
> 511	      method: "GET",
> 512	      headers: { Authorization: authHeader, Accept: "application/json" },
> 513	    });
> 514	    if (!statusResp.ok) {
> 515	      const t = await statusResp.text();
> 516	      throw new Error(`Higgsfield status error ${statusResp.status}: ${t.slice(0, 200)}`);
> 517	    }
> 518	    const statusData = await statusResp.json();
> 519	    if (statusData.status === "completed") {
> 520	      const url = statusData.images?.[0]?.url || statusData.video?.url || "";
> 521	      if (!url) throw new Error("Higgsfield: geração concluída sem URL de saída");
> 522	      return { url };
> 523	    }
> 524	    if (statusData.status === "failed") throw new Error("Higgsfield: a geração falhou no provedor");
> 525	    if (statusData.status === "nsfw") throw new Error("Higgsfield: conteúdo bloqueado por moderação");
> 526	    if (statusData.status === "cancelled") throw new Error("Higgsfield: geração cancelada");
> 527	  }
> 528	  throw new Error("Higgsfield: geração demorou demais (timeout >90s)");
> 529	}
> 530	
> ```

> 1	# Proposal — Phase 4.2b Async Video Webhook (vm_canvas_jobs vs. extend vm_canvas_executions)
> 2	
> 3	| Field | Value |
> 4	|---|---|
> 5	| **Date** | 2026-05-16 |

> 11	---
> 12	
> 13	## Major revision vs. original plan
> 14	
> 15	O plano original (em `magical-baking-trinket.md` Task 4.2) propunha **criar uma tabela nova `vm_canvas_jobs`** com 16 colunas. Verificação revelou que **`vm_canvas_executions` (migration `20260514040200_viralmind_canvas_studio.sql`) já existe e cobre 80% do schema necessário** — tem `provider`, `model`, `parameters`, `output_url`, `credits_charged`, `status`, `error_message`, timestamps. Criar `vm_canvas_jobs` separado introduziria duas fontes de verdade para jobs Canvas (sync + async), gerando débito de manutenção.
> 16	
> 17	**Decisão revisada: EXTEND `vm_canvas_executions` com 4 colunas + 1 status novo.**
> 18	
> 19	---
> 20	
> 21	## Problem statement
> 22	
> 23	Phase 4.2b (Higgsfield video routes — DoP Lite/Turbo/Standard, Kling 2.1 Pro, Seedance v1) precisa de:
> 24	
> 25	1. **Async job tracking:** vídeo gen leva 30s–10min — polling síncrono dentro de Edge Function ultrapassa o timeout do Supabase Functions.
> 26	2. **Webhook callback:** Higgsfield API espera `?hf_webhook=URL` query param (NÃO body — confirmado em `.claude/context/higgsfield-api-validation-2026-05-15.md`).
> 27	3. **Webhook authentication:** Higgsfield NÃO oferece HMAC nativo; recomendação dos docs é "token na URL".
> 28	4. **Débito atômico delayed:** mcoCoins só descontados após **upload-OK** do vídeo final no `canvas-assets` bucket (caso webhook chegue mas download falhe, refund é zero — nunca cobrou).
> 29	5. **Frontend realtime updates:** React quer ver status badge mudar (queued → running → done) sem polling do cliente.
> 30	
> 31	Current state:
> 32	- `canvas-execute/index.ts:430` tem TODO inline citando esta spec.
> 33	- `vm_canvas_executions.status` enum tem `'pending','queued','running','success','failed','cancelled'` — adequado.
> 34	- Nenhuma coluna para webhook token; nenhuma coluna para `operation_id` upstream; nenhum método para Realtime channel filter.
> 35	
> 36	---
> 37	
> 38	## Recommended path — extend `vm_canvas_executions`
> 39	
> 40	### Migration: `20260516HHMMSS_vm_canvas_executions_async_video.sql`
> 41	
> 42	```sql
> 43	-- Phase 4.2b async video tracking: webhook callback + operation polling fallback
> 44	-- Aligned with .claude/context/higgsfield-api-validation-2026-05-15.md
> 45	
> 46	-- 1. New columns on existing table
> 47	ALTER TABLE public.vm_canvas_executions
> 48	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS operation_id   TEXT,
> 49	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS webhook_token  TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT encode(gen_random_bytes(32), 'hex'),
> 50	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS response_payload JSONB,
> 51	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS webhook_received_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
> 52	
> 53	-- 2. Extend status enum with 'timeout' (gen took > 10min without webhook + polling exhausted)
> 54	ALTER TABLE public.vm_canvas_executions DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS vm_canvas_executions_status_check;
> 55	ALTER TABLE public.vm_canvas_executions ADD CONSTRAINT vm_canvas_executions_status_check
> 56	  CHECK (status IN ('pending','queued','running','success','failed','cancelled','timeout'));
> 57	
> 58	-- 3. Webhook token must be unique per execution (defense against token replay across executions)
> 59	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_vm_canvas_executions_webhook_token
> 60	  ON public.vm_canvas_executions(webhook_token);
> 61	
> 62	-- 4. Index for active polling watchdog (rare query — partial index)
> 63	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_vm_canvas_executions_pending_polling
> 64	  ON public.vm_canvas_executions(operation_id, status, started_at)
> 65	  WHERE status IN ('queued','running') AND operation_id IS NOT NULL;
> 66	
> 67	-- 5. Update RLS — service_role still owns writes; users can read their executions (already in place per migration 040200).
> 68	-- New: explicitly deny user UPDATEs on webhook_token / operation_id / response_payload (service-role only).
> 69	-- Existing policy "Service role manages executions" already covers all writes; nothing new needed.
> 70	
> 71	-- 6. Realtime: enable replication for vm_canvas_executions so client can subscribe via Supabase Realtime.
> 72	ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.vm_canvas_executions;
> 73	```
> 74	
> 75	**Why not separate vm_canvas_jobs table?** See "Major revision" header. `vm_canvas_executions` already differentiates sync vs async naturally — sync runs complete in-function (status goes `pending → running → success` within seconds); async runs sit in `queued/running` waiting for webhook callback OR polling watchdog.
> 76	
> 77	### Edge Function: `higgsfield-webhook` (new, ~200 LOC)
> 78	
> 79	```typescript
> 80	// supabase/functions/higgsfield-webhook/index.ts
> 81	//
> 82	// Receives Higgsfield video generation callbacks.
> 83	// URL pattern: https://<project>.supabase.co/functions/v1/higgsfield-webhook?token=<webhook_token>
> 84	//
> 85	// Pipeline:
> 86	//   1. Extract `?token=` query param
> 87	//   2. Lookup vm_canvas_executions WHERE webhook_token = token (atomic; unique index)
> 88	//   3. Validate body payload + status code from Higgsfield
> 89	//   4. Download video bytes from result URL (reuse pattern from rescue-video:39-77)
> 90	//      - Validate size ≥ 100 KB (rejects JSON error stubs masquerading as videos)
> 91	//      - Validate Content-Type starts with 'video/'
> 92	//   5. Upload to canvas-assets bucket (private; signed URL 7d, like vm_canvas_assets pattern)
> 93	//   6. Atomic transaction:
> 94	//        a. vm_canvas_executions: UPDATE status='success', output_url=<signed>,
> 95	//           response_payload=<body>, webhook_received_at=NOW(), completed_at=NOW()
> 96	//        b. deduct_mco_coins(user_id, credits_charged) — only AFTER upload OK
> 97	//        c. INSERT INTO vm_canvas_assets (project_id, user_id, ...) for the new video
> 98	//   7. If any step fails: status='failed', error_message=<reason>; mcoCoins NOT charged.
> 99	//
> 100	// Security:
> 101	//   - JWT NOT required (Higgsfield is external caller). Auth is via webhook_token in URL.
> 102	//   - Token is per-execution (unique index) and 32-byte random.
> 103	//   - Token leak → attacker can complete ONE execution maliciously; mitigated by:
> 104	//      a. Token expires after 1 use (status check: must be 'queued' or 'running' to accept callback)
> 105	//      b. Token rotation via DEFAULT encode(gen_random_bytes(32), 'hex') on every INSERT
> 106	//   - Service role used internally for writes; user_id derived from execution row.
> 107	```
> 108	
> 109	### Frontend hook: `useCanvasJobsRealtime` (new, ~80 LOC)
> 110	
> 111	```typescript
> 112	// src/hooks/useCanvasJobsRealtime.ts
> 113	//
> 114	// Subscribes to vm_canvas_executions changes for the current user via Supabase Realtime.
> 115	// On status change: invalidates TanStack Query keys ['canvas-execution', id] and
> 116	// (if status='success') ['canvas-assets', project_id].
> 117	//
> 118	// Usage in CanvasEditorPage:
> 119	//   useCanvasJobsRealtime();  // top-level hook, no args needed (filters by user via RLS)
> 120	//
> 121	// Realtime channel filter:
> 122	//   channel('vm_canvas_executions:user')
> 123	//     .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'UPDATE', schema: 'public', table: 'vm_canvas_executions',
> 124	//          filter: `user_id=eq.${userId}` }, handler)
> 125	//
> 126	// Cleanup: unmount removes channel via supabase.removeChannel(ch).
> 127	// Reconnect: TanStack Query handles re-fetch on focus naturally.
> 128	```
> 129	
> 130	### canvas-execute changes (`supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts`)
> 131	
> 132	Para video providers (DoP/Kling/Seedance):
> 133	
> 134	1. INSERT vm_canvas_executions com status='queued', `credits_charged` calculado mas NÃO deduzido.
> 135	2. POST Higgsfield API com `?hf_webhook=https://<project>.supabase.co/functions/v1/higgsfield-webhook?token=<row.webhook_token>` no URL — webhook é GET (the validation doc confirmou); ou POST se vier resposta inicial.
> 136	3. Capturar `operation_id` da resposta inicial; UPDATE vm_canvas_executions.operation_id.
> 137	4. Retornar `{ executionId: row.id, status: 'queued', estimatedSeconds: N }` ao frontend.
> 138	5. Cliente subscreve via `useCanvasJobsRealtime` e renderiza badge.
> 139	
> 140	Para image providers (Higgsfield Soul, OpenAI DALL·E, Replicate FLUX): **path síncrono atual permanece intocado** (status='pending'→'running'→'success' dentro da mesma function call).

> 144	## Watchdog cron (defense-in-depth — Phase 4.2c)
> 145	
> 146	Caso webhook NUNCA chegue (Higgsfield down, network blip, token corrupted):
> 147	
> 148	```bash
> 149	# 5 * * * * — every 5 min, check executions stale (queued/running > 15min) com operation_id
> 150	# Polls Higgsfield API directly; updates row to 'success'/'failed' OR sets status='timeout' if no progress > 60min.
> 151	*/5 * * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/canvas-jobs-poll-fallback.sh
> 152	```
> 153	
> 154	(Scope desta proposal: spec only. Cron criado na Phase 4.2c.)

> 157	
> 158	## Atomic debit semantic — critical clarification
> 159	
> 160	**Current `deduct_mco_coins(user_id, amount)`** decrements `profiles.mco_balance` synchronously com NO transaction log (per Task 4.1 audit). For Phase 4.2b, **the deduct happens INSIDE the webhook handler** only after:
> 161	1. Video bytes successfully downloaded (≥ 100 KB)
> 162	2. Uploaded to canvas-assets bucket
> 163	3. Signed URL generated and stored
> 164	
> 165	If ANY of these fail → status='failed' or 'error' AND zero deduct. User never charged for failed gens. Aligns with Sovereign's "atomic só-após-upload-OK" requirement from the original plan.
> 166	
> 167	**Cross-dependency:** if Sovereign approves **Option 3 of Task 4.1 (deduct logs to mcoin_transactions)**, the webhook deduct will automatically log a `'spend'` ledger entry — desbloqueando audit financeiro for Phase Commercial.

> 174	2. **Cancellation:** se usuário fechar a página com job em `running`, devemos chamar API de cancel da Higgsfield (recupera créditos prepaid) ou deixar terminar?

> 259	      if (error) throw error;
> 260	      return (data || []) as CanvasExecution[];
> 261	    },
> 262	    enabled: !!user,
> 263	  });
> 264	}
> 265	
> 266	// ── Recipes ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 267	
> 268	export interface CanvasRecipe {
> 269	  id: string;
> 270	  user_id: string | null;
> 271	  name: string;
> 272	  description: string | null;
> 273	  thumbnail_url: string | null;
> 274	  graph: CanvasGraph;
> 275	  estimated_credits: number;
> 276	  category: string | null;
> 277	  tags: string[];
> 278	  is_public: boolean;
> 279	  is_official: boolean;
> 280	  uses_count: number;
> 281	  created_at: string;
> 282	}
> 283	
> 284	/** Public + own recipes. RLS gates the result to is_public OR auth.uid() = user_id. */
> 285	export function useCanvasRecipes() {
> 286	  return useQuery({
> 287	    queryKey: ["vm-canvas-recipes"],
> 288	    queryFn: async () => {
> 289	      const { data, error } = await supabase
> 290	        .from("vm_canvas_recipes")
> 291	        .select("*")
> 292	        .order("is_official", { ascending: false })
> 293	        .order("uses_count", { ascending: false });
> 294	      if (error) throw error;
> 295	      return (data || []) as CanvasRecipe[];
> 296	    },
> 297	    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
> 298	  });
> 299	}
> 300	
> 301	// ── Project metadata ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 302	
> 303	export function useUpdateCanvasProject() {
> 304	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
> 305	
> 306	  return useMutation({
> 307	    mutationFn: async ({ projectId, name, description }: {
> 308	      projectId: string;
> 309	      name?: string;
> 310	      description?: string;
> 311	    }) => {
> 312	      const patch: Record<string, string> = {};
> 313	      if (name !== undefined) patch.name = name;
> 314	      if (description !== undefined) patch.description = description;
> 315	      const { error } = await supabase
> 316	        .from("vm_canvas_projects")
> 317	        .update(patch)
> 318	        .eq("id", projectId);
> 319	      if (error) throw error;
> 320	    },
> 321	    onSuccess: (_, vars) => {
> 322	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-project", vars.projectId] });
> 323	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-projects"] });
> 324	    },
> 325	  });
> 326	}

> 21	## Base URL & Auth (✅ alinhado)
> 22

> 32	
> 33	## Endpoints canônicos
> 34	
> 35	### Queue management (✅ alinhado)
> 36	
> 37	| Endpoint | Method | Uso |
> 38	|----------|--------|-----|
> 39	| `POST /{model_id}` | POST | Submit generation request |
> 40	| `GET /requests/{request_id}/status` | GET | Poll status |
> 41	| `POST /requests/{request_id}/cancel` | POST | Cancel (apenas em `queued`) |
> 42	
> 43	`{model_id}` é o caminho completo após o host (e.g. `higgsfield-ai/soul/standard`).
> 44	
> 45	### Modelos relevantes para Canvas Studio
> 46	
> 47	| Caminho canônico | Tipo | Uso no Canvas |
> 48	|------------------|------|---------------|
> 49	| `higgsfield-ai/soul/standard` | text-to-image | GenerateImage node (modelo premium) |
> 50	| `higgsfield-ai/dop/standard` | image-to-video | ImageToVideo node (qualidade max) |
> 51	| `higgsfield-ai/dop/preview` | **alias** deprecado | NÃO usar (legado) |

Preview)"** — `preview` foi renomeado para `standard`. Usar `standard` em produção; `preview` provavelmente continua respondendo mas é nomenclatura legada.

Recomendação: usar `image_url` + `prompt` + `duration` no v1; investigar params avançados via `/docs/api-reference` numa Phase 4.3 follow-up.
> 97	
> 98	---
> 99	
> 100	## Response shape
> 101	
> 102	### Queued (HTTP 200)
> 103	```json
> 104	{
> 105	  "status": "queued",
> 106	  "request_id": "d7e6c0f3-6699-4f6c-bb45-2ad7fd9158ff",
> 107	  "status_url": "https://platform.higgsfield.ai/requests/.../status",
> 108	  "cancel_url": "https://platform.higgsfield.ai/requests/.../cancel"
> 109	}
> 110	```
> 111	
> 112	### Completed (poll ou webhook)
> 113	```json
> 114	{
> 115	  "status": "completed",
> 116	  "request_id": "...",
> 117	  "status_url": "...",
> 118	  "cancel_url": "...",
> 119	  "images": [{ "url": "..." }],      // se image gen
> 120	  "video":  { "url": "..." }          // se video gen
> 121	}
> 122	```
> 123	
> 124	### Failed / NSFW
> 125	```json
> 126	{
> 127	  "status": "failed" | "nsfw",
> 128	  "request_id": "...",
> 129	  "error": "Generation fail message"    // apenas em failed
> 130	}
> 131	```
> 132	
> 133	### Estados possíveis
> 134	
> 135	| Status | Significado | Crédito |
> 136	|--------|-------------|---------|
> 137	| `queued` | Na fila | reservado |
> 138	| `in_progress` | Processando (não-cancelável) | reservado |
> 139	| `completed` | Concluído com sucesso | **cobrado** |
> 140	| `failed` | Erro técnico | **refund automático** |
> 141	| `nsfw` | Bloqueado por moderação | **refund automático** |
> 142	
> 143	✅ **Confirma o invariante do plano:** debitar `mco_balance` SOMENTE após `completed` + upload OK. Failed/NSFW = sem cobrança (refund é automático no lado Higgsfield).
> 144	
> 145	---
> 146	
> 147	## Webhook spec — DIVERGÊNCIA CRÍTICA
> 148	
> 149	### Como passar a URL do webhook ❗
> 150	
> 151	**Não é via body.** A URL é passada como **query parameter** `hf_webhook` na URL do submit:
> 152	
> 153	```bash
> 154	POST 'https://platform.higgsfield.ai/higgsfield-ai/soul/standard?hf_webhook=https://my.endpoint/callback'
> 155	Authorization: Key {key}:{secret}
> 156	...
> 157	```
> 158	
> 159	**Implicação no código:**
> 160	
> 161	```typescript
> 162	// _shared/higgsfield.ts (Deno) — pseudocódigo
> 163	async function submitVideoJob(opts: {
> 164	  modelPath: string;       // "higgsfield-ai/dop/standard"
> 165	  imageUrl: string;
> 166	  prompt: string;
> 167	  duration: number;
> 168	  webhookUrl: string;
> 169	  apiKey: string;
> 170	  apiSecret: string;
> 171	}) {
> 172	  const submitUrl = new URL(`https://platform.higgsfield.ai/${opts.modelPath}`);
> 173	  submitUrl.searchParams.set("hf_webhook", opts.webhookUrl);   // ← query param
> 174	
> 175	  const res = await fetch(submitUrl.toString(), {
> 176	    method: "POST",
> 177	    headers: {
> 178	      "Authorization": `Key ${opts.apiKey}:${opts.apiSecret}`,
> 179	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
> 180	      "Accept": "application/json",
> 181	    },
> 182	    body: JSON.stringify({
> 183	      image_url: opts.imageUrl,
> 184	      prompt: opts.prompt,
> 185	      duration: opts.duration,
> 186	    }),
> 187	  });
> 188	  // ...
> 189	}
> 190	```
> 191	
> 192	### Webhook payload (recebido pelo Edge Function MCORCH)
> 193	
> 194	POST request, JSON body, **sem assinatura HMAC** (docs não mencionam nenhum mecanismo de signing).
> 195	
> 196	```json
> 197	// Completed (image)
> 198	{ "status": "completed", "request_id": "...", "status_url": "...", "cancel_url": "...", "images": [{"url": "..."}] }
> 199	
> 200	// Completed (video)
> 201	{ "status": "completed", "request_id": "...", "status_url": "...", "cancel_url": "...", "video": {"url": "..."} }
> 202	
> 203	// Failed
> 204	{ "status": "failed", "request_id": "...", "error": "..." }
> 205	
> 206	// NSFW
> 207	{ "status": "nsfw", "request_id": "..." }
> 208	```
> 209	
> 210	### Retry policy
> 211	
> 212	- **2 horas** de retry até resposta 2xx
> 213	- Se endpoint continuar falhando, retries cessam — `status_url` continua disponível para polling manual
> 214	- **MUST** retornar `2xx` para acknowledgment (sob 10s)
> 215	
> 216	### Idempotência
> 217	
> 218	- Higgsfield pode entregar o mesmo webhook múltiplas vezes durante retries
> 219	- **Edge Function MUST** ser idempotente via `request_id`:
> 220	  ```sql
> 221	  -- pseudocódigo
> 222	  UPDATE vm_canvas_jobs
> 223	  SET status = 'completed', result_url = ..., updated_at = NOW()
> 224	  WHERE higgsfield_request_id = $1 AND status = 'pending';
> 225	  -- Se status já = 'completed', UPDATE retorna 0 rows e nada acontece
> 226	  ```
> 227	
> 228	### ⚠️ Webhook auth — Higgsfield NÃO assina o callback
> 229	
> 230	**Recomendação MCORCH:** proteger o endpoint via **token embutido na URL** do webhook:
> 231	
> 232	```typescript
> 233	// Quando faz submit:
> 234	const webhookUrl = `${Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")}/functions/v1/higgsfield-webhook?token=${Deno.env.get("HIGGSFIELD_WEBHOOK_SECRET")}`;
> 235	```
> 236	
> 237	```typescript
> 238	// No edge function higgsfield-webhook:
> 239	const token = new URL(req.url).searchParams.get("token");
> 240	if (token !== Deno.env.get("HIGGSFIELD_WEBHOOK_SECRET")) {
> 241	  return new Response("Forbidden", { status: 403 });
> 242	}
> 243	```
> 244	
> 245	`HIGGSFIELD_WEBHOOK_SECRET` é um **secret novo no vault Supabase**, gerado uma vez (ex: `openssl rand -hex 32`). Engineer agent é owner desse secret.
> 246

> 248	
> 249	## Pricing (CONFIRMADO via /docs/pricing)
> 250	
> 251	**Conversão:** `$1 USD = 16 credits` · pay-as-you-go · sem mínimo de volume
> 252	
> 253	### Soul (Text-to-Image)
> 254	
> 255	| Quality | Batch | Credits | USD | Comentário |
> 256	|---------|-------|---------|-----|-----|
> 257	| 720p | 1 | 1.5 | $0.09 | menor custo de validação |

> 279

> 281	- `cloud.higgsfield.ai/explore` (Models Gallery)

> 286	
> 287	---
> 288	
> 289	## Cobrança & Refund (✅ alinhado com o plano)
> 290	
> 291	- ✅ **Failed/NSFW = refund automático** no lado Higgsfield. Cliente MCORCH só precisa NÃO debitar `mco_balance` quando webhook chegar com status != `completed`.
> 292	- ✅ Sovereign não paga por requests cancelados via `/cancel` em `queued`.
> 293	- ✅ Arquivos resultantes **só retidos por 7 dias** no Higgsfield. **MCORCH precisa baixar imediatamente após `completed`** para o bucket `canvas-assets` (lifetime do Sovereign).
> 294	
> 295	### Invariante de billing (alinhado com `floating-sleeping-sparrow.md`)
> 296	
> 297	```
> 298	Submit (canvas-execute):
> 299	  └── INSERT vm_canvas_jobs (status='pending', NÃO debita mco_balance)
> 300	
> 301	Webhook (higgsfield-webhook):
> 302	  ├── status='completed':
> 303	  │     ├── Download bytes do result_url Higgsfield
> 304	  │     ├── Upload para bucket 'canvas-assets'  (se falhar → status='failed', sem débito)
> 305	  │     ├── deduct_mco_coins(user_id, coin_cost)   ← débito atômico aqui
> 306	  │     ├── INSERT vm_canvas_executions + vm_canvas_assets
> 307	  │     └── UPDATE vm_canvas_jobs (status='completed', result_url=...)
> 308	  │
> 309	  ├── status='failed' OU 'nsfw':
> 310	  │     └── UPDATE vm_canvas_jobs (status=<status>, error=...)
> 311	  │     (Higgsfield refund é automático; nada a fazer no nosso lado)
> 312	  │
> 313	  └── (Idempotência: UPDATE somente quando job.status='pending')
> 314	```
> 315	
> 316	---
> 317	
> 318	## Best Practices observadas (consolidar no client Deno)
> 319	
> 320	1. **Polling fallback:** se webhook falhar (2h sem 2xx), client deve poder fazer poll manual de `status_url`. Engineer agent monitora `vm_canvas_jobs.status='pending'` com `created_at > 2h ago` e dispara poll de recovery.
> 321	2. **Resposta rápida do webhook:** ≤ 10s para acknowledgment. Não fazer upload síncrono dentro do handler — usar `queueMicrotask` ou Supabase Edge Background Tasks.
> 322	3. **HTTPS obrigatório** — Supabase Edge Functions já HTTPS por padrão. ✅
> 323	4. **`request_id` como identidade canônica** — usar como chave primária de idempotência em `vm_canvas_jobs.higgsfield_request_id` (UNIQUE constraint na migration).

> 586	/**

> 594	): {

> 600	  parameters: Record<string, unknown>;
> 601	} | null {

> 603	    case "generateImage": {

> 608	        seed: data.fixedSeed && data.seed != null ? data.seed : undefined,

> 610	      if (info.resolution) parameters.resolution = info.resolution;

> 615	        model: info.modelParam,
> 616	        prompt: data.prompt,
> 617	        negative_prompt: data.negativePrompt || undefined,
> 618	        parameters,
> 619	      };
> 620	    }
> 621	    case "sceneCompose": {
> 622	      const info = MODEL_INFO[data.modelId];
> 623	      const parameters: Record<string, unknown> = {
> 624	        aspect_ratio: data.aspectRatio,
> 625	      };
> 626	      if (info.resolution) parameters.resolution = info.resolution;

> 632	        prompt: data.prompt,

PostgREST query: `GET ${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/vm_canvas_executions?status=in.(queued,running)&operation_id=not.is.null&created_at=lt.${ts_10min_ago}` (URL-encode `ts_10min_ago = $(date -u -d \'10 minutes ago\' +%FT%TZ)`).\n  3. For each row: curl `GET ${BASE_URL}/requests/${operation_id}/status` with `Authorization: Key ${HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY}:${HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY_SECRET}`.\n  4. Branch on response status:\n     - `completed` → curl POST webhook URL constructed from row.webhook_token with the Higgsfield body (manual fallback delivery; webhook is idempotent via 409 check).\n     - `failed` / `nsfw` / `cancelled` → curl PATCH PostgREST to set status=\'failed\' + error_message.\n     - Still queued/in_progress AND `(now - started_at) > 60min` → PATCH status=\'timeout\' (no refund needed; no deduct happened).\n  5. Log to `/var/log/mcorch-canvas-watchdog.log` (per-run summary).\n  6. Health pulse INSERT into `infra_health_logs` with `service: \'canvas-video-watchdog\'`.\n- Add to crontab: `*/5 * * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-canvas-watchdog.log 2>&1`.\n- Document install command in SOP (Recovery section).\n\n### G. `docs/processes/canvas-video-async-execution.md` (NEW, ~140 LOC; low risk)\n\nContent per §2 outline.\n\n## 4. > 
> **Localização**: após line 187, ANTES do dispatch atual `if (body.provider === "higgsfield")`.
> 
> ```typescript
> if (body.node_type === "image_to_video") {
>   return await submitHiggsfieldVideo(admin, supabaseUrl, keys, body, execution);
> }
> ```
> 
> Nova função `submitHiggsfieldVideo()`:
> 1. Resolve `input_asset_url` do body (frontend deve passar — ver Step 4).
> 2. Build webhook URL: `${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/higgsfield-webhook?token=${execution.webhook_token}`.
> 3. Build submit URL: `${BASE_URL}/higgsfield-ai/dop/standard?hf_webhook=${encodeURIComponent(webhookUrl)}` (Conservador — apenas dop/standard).
> 4. POST com `Authorization: Key apiKey:apiSecret`, body `{image_url, prompt, duration, motion_strength}`.
> 5. Non-2xx → UPDATE exec status='failed', return 502.
> 6. 2xx → capturar `request_id`, UPDATE `vm_canvas_executions SET operation_id = request_id, status = 'queued' WHERE id = execution.id`.
> 7. Health pulse `infra_health_logs(service='canvas-execute', status='healthy')`.
> 8. Return `{ execution_id, status: 'queued', webhook_token, operation_id }`. **Sem deduct** — débito é atômico no webhook após upload OK.
> 
> Daily cap pre-check existente (linha 134-157) **fica intacta** — soma histórica canvas_*_spend; jobs queued-but-not-deducted não duplam.
> 
> Deploy: `npx supabase functions deploy canvas-execute`.
> 
> ### Step 3 — `src/hooks/useCanvasJobsRealtime.ts` callback extension
> 
> Extender assinatura: `useCanvasJobsRealtime(projectId?: string, onUpdate?: (row: ExecutionRow) => void)`.
> 
> Dentro do channel handler (após invalidações), `onUpdate?.(payload.new as ExecutionRow)`. Documentar no JSDoc que o callback é o ponto de sincronização Realtime → Zustand store.
> 
> ### Step 4 — `src/pages/CanvasEditorPage.tsx` wiring
> 
> 1. Importar `useCanvasJobsRealtime` + `pickUpstreamImage` (extraído como util).
> 2. Em `CanvasShell`, chamar:
> ```typescript
> useCanvasJobsRealtime(projectId, (row) => {
>   if (!row.node_id) return;
>   if (row.status === 'success') {
>     updateNodeData(row.node_id, {
>       status: 'success',
>       output: { videoUrl: row.output_url, generatedAt: row.completed_at },
>       error: undefined,
>     });
>     refetchDashboard?.();
>   } else if (row.status === 'failed' || row.status === 'timeout') {
>     updateNodeData(row.node_id, { status: 'failed', error: row.error_message ?? row.status });
>   } else if (row.status === 'queued' || row.status === 'running') {
>     updateNodeData(row.node_id, { status: 'running' });
>   }
> });
> ```
> 3. `isRunnable()` (linha 73) — remover bloqueio de imageToVideo, substituir por check de upstream image resolvable.
> 4. `handleRunAll()` — branch para imageToVideo: após `mutateAsync`, deixar status='running' (NÃO `success`), incrementar ok como "submetido". Toast adapta: "X nós · Y vídeos aguardando webhook".
> 5. Resolver `input_asset_url` no call-site (Run-All + inspector) usando `pickUpstreamImage` antes de passar ao `executeNode.mutateAsync`. Manter `resolveExecutePayload` puro.
> 
> ### Step 5 — `src/components/canvas/RightPanel/inspectors/ImageToVideoInspector.tsx` Run button
> 
> 1. Importar `useCanvasExecute`, `resolveExecutePayload`, `toast`.
> 2. Adicionar `onCreditsChanged?: () => void` em Props.
> 3. `const execute = useCanvasExecute();`
> 4. `handleExecute` (mirror lines 80-105 do `GenerateImageInspector.tsx`): set status='running' antes do submit, sem set output.videoUrl no resolve (Realtime cuida), toast `"Job enviado — aguardando webhook (eta ${info.eta})"`.
> 5. Replace bottom warning (lines 101-110) por info softer: `"Job assíncrono — UI atualiza via webhook em ~${info.eta}."`.
> 6. Replace disabled Run (lines 244-262): `onClick={handleExecute}`, `disabled={insufficient || !sourceImageUrl || !node.data.prompt.trim() || execute.isPending || node.data.status === 'running'}`, label `"Gerar vídeo · ${cost} 💎"`.
> 7. **Catálogo MODELS** (linha 33-39): manter os 5 entries, mas adicionar prop `disabled` em cada button visual para Lite/Turbo/Kling/Seedance + tooltip "Aguardando validação tier — Phase 4.3". Apenas `dop-standard-5s` clicável.
> 
> ### Step 6 — `scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh` (NEW, ~80 LOC)
> 
> Padrão `scripts/auto-publish-cron.sh` + `scripts/watchdog-mcorch.sh`. Bash:
> 1. Carrega `.env` (SUPABASE_URL + SERVICE_ROLE_KEY + HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY + secret).
> 2. PostgREST query: `GET ${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/vm_canvas_executions?status=in.(queued,running)&operation_id=not.is.null&created_at=lt.${ts_10min_ago}`.
> 3. Para cada row: curl `GET ${BASE_URL}/requests/${operation_id}/status` com auth Higgsfield.
> 4. Branch:
>    - `completed` → POST webhook URL manualmente (idempotente via 409 check).
>    - `failed`/`nsfw`/`cancelled` → PATCH PostgREST status='failed' + error_message.
>    - Queued/in_progress + `started_at > 60min` → PATCH status='timeout' (sem refund — deduct nunca aconteceu).
> 5. Log `/var/log/mcorch-canvas-watchdog.log`.
> 6. Health pulse `infra_health_logs(service='canvas-video-watchdog')`.
> 
> Crontab: `*/5 * * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-canvas-watchdog.log 2>&1`.

>

>

> git log --oneline docs/processes/canvas-video-async-execution.md | head -1
>

>

> bun run build  # → confirma chunk size delta razoável
> 
> # 4. Smoke test pago (Sovereign executa):
> #    a. /dashboard/canvas/<projectId> → GenerateImage "neon skyline" → Run → imageUrl
> #    b. ImageToVideo downstream → conectar edge → dop-standard-5s, duration=5s, prompt "slow dolly-in", motion=0.5 → Run
> #    c. Capturar HAR: POST canvas-execute → 200 com {execution_id, status: 'queued', webhook_token}
> 
> # 5. DB material proof (< 10s pós-submit):
> psql -c "SELECT id, status, operation_id, started_at, webhook_token
>          FROM vm_canvas_executions WHERE id = '<uuid>';"
> # Esperado: status='queued', operation_id NOT NULL, webhook_token 64 hex
> 
> # 6. Higgsfield in-flight (< 30s):
> curl -H "Authorization: Key $K:$S" "https://platform.higgsfield.ai/requests/<operation_id>/status"
> # Esperado: status ∈ {queued, in_progress, completed}
> 
> # 7. Webhook completion (< 2min):
> psql -c "SELECT status, output_url, webhook_received_at, completed_at,
>                 response_payload->>'request_id' AS hf_req
>          FROM vm_canvas_executions WHERE id = '<uuid>';"
> # Esperado: status='success', output_url signed, completed_at NOT NULL
> 
> # 8. Asset row:
> psql -c "SELECT id, file_size_bytes, mime_type, storage_key FROM vm_canvas_assets
>          WHERE storage_key = '<project_id>/<execution_id>.mp4';"
> # Esperado: 1 row, ≥100 KB, mime_type LIKE 'video/%'
> 
> # 9. Ledger deduct atômico:
> psql -c "SELECT amount, action, context FROM mcoin_transactions
>          WHERE context->>'execution_id' = '<uuid>' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1;"
> # Esperado: amount=-125, action='canvas_video_spend'
> 
> # 10. Realtime UI:
> #     DevTools → Network → WS → channel canvas_jobs_<user_id> → UPDATE status='success'
> #     Browser: <video> element renderiza no ImageToVideoNode sem refresh
> 
> # 11. Watchdog dry-run (no in-flight):
> bash scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh
> # Esperado: log "0 jobs to check" + insert em infra_health_logs
> 
> # 12. Watchdog recovery (simulado):
> psql -c "UPDATE vm_canvas_executions SET started_at = NOW() - INTERVAL '70 minutes'
>          WHERE id = '<test-uuid>' AND status = 'queued';"
> bash scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh
> # Esperado: status flips to 'timeout' + log line
> ```

> 
> Se smoke test queimar créditos inesperadamente:
> 1. `git checkout HEAD~1 supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts && npx supabase functions deploy canvas-execute` — reverte branch async, falls back para sync poll que rejeita vídeo via 90s timeout (zero credit burn possível).
> 2. Revert `ImageToVideoInspector.tsx` (1 file) — UI volta para "Aguardando Phase 4.2b".
> 3. `crontab -e` comenta `canvas-video-watchdog` line.
> 4. Migration **NÃO** é revertida (colunas são aditivas, não quebram sync path).
> 5. Sovereign manual refund via `award_mco_coins` se necessário (Higgsfield auto-refunda failed/nsfw).
> 
> ```

> **Tool Result (Read)** - ✅ Success
>
> ```
> 1	# SOP — Canvas Daily Cap Handling
> 2	
> 3	**Versão:** v1 · **Selada:** 2026-05-17 · **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado)** · **SSP-01 OE04**
> 4	
> 5	## ORO triplet
> 6	
> 7	- **Operator:** end user (gera no Canvas Studio); admin (overrides — Phase 5)
> 8	- **Reviewer:** Sovereign (revisa policy quando cap atingido por user pagante)
> 9	- **Owner:** Sovereign (cost discipline owner — Pillar 3)
> 10	
> 11	## Contexto
> 12	
> 13	`canvas-execute` agora aplica um **Daily Cap de 100 mcoCoins** por usuário (janela rolante de 24h). Quando o user tenta gerar e o cap seria estourado, o backend retorna HTTP 402 com JSON estruturado em vez de cobrar. Esta SOP cobre o fluxo de **reconhecimento + comunicação + recovery** para o usuário e o operador.
> 14	
> 15	**Por que existe:** Survival Audit v1 escorou Cost Discipline 2/5. Sem cap, um bug ou abuse case poderia drenar todo o saldo de um user em minutos (5 falhas Higgsfield em 30s = 130 mcoCoins ≈ R$ 10 perdidos). Cap = blast radius limitado + previsibilidade financeira.
> 16	
> 17	**Threshold rationale:** 100 mcoCoins/dia ≈ $8 USD (markup 13× sobre custo nominal Higgsfield). Permite ~5 vídeos Soul 1080p_4 (65 each) OU ~25 imagens DALL·E 3 (15 each) por dia. Suficiente para validação criativa de Usuário Zero; insuficiente para abuse.
> 18	
> 19	## Resposta HTTP quando cap atingido
> 20	
> 21	```json
> 22	HTTP 402 Payment Required
> 23	{
> 24	  "error": "Daily Canvas cap reached",
> 25	  "cap": 100,
> 26	  "spent_today": 95,
> 27	  "required": 20,
> 28	  "resets_at": "2026-05-18T05:00:00.000Z"
> 29	}
> 30	```
> 31	
> 32	## Sequence — fluxo do usuário
> 33	
> 34	| # | Action | Output esperado | Verification gate |
> 35	|---|--------|-----------------|-------------------|
> 36	| 1 | User dispara generation no Canvas que estouraria cap | Frontend recebe HTTP 402 com JSON acima | `error: "Daily Canvas cap reached"` |
> 37	| 2 | Frontend renderiza toast: "Cap diário atingido (95/100 mcoCoins). Reseta em <X>h." | Sonner toast visível, duração ≥6s | `id: "canvas-cap-reached"` (dedupe) |
> 38	| 3 | User aguarda janela rolante reset (próxima execução >24h após a primeira do dia) | Próxima generation cobra normal | spent_today drops below `cap` |
> 39	| 4 | (Se user pagante reclamar) Operator analisa `mcoin_transactions` filtrado por `action LIKE 'canvas_%'` last 24h | Lista de gastos com timestamps | sum = `spent_today` reportado |
> 40	| 5 | (Decisão Sovereign) override manual: insert linha `award_mco_coins` com `action='canvas_cap_override'` para zerar contagem efetiva | UUID returned | next call passes cap check |
> 41	
> 42	## Verification gates
> 43	
> 44	- Cap check ANTES do deduct: garante zero cobrança quando bloqueado.
> 45	- Action tagging consistente: TODA chamada `canvas-execute` em sucesso usa `canvas_image_spend` ou `canvas_video_spend`. Sem tags, query do cap não conta.
> 46	- Reset implícito: query usa `created_at >= now() - interval '24 hours'`. Não há cron de reset (janela rolante > janela calendário).
> 47	
> 48	## Recovery path
> 49	
> 50	- **Cap bloqueia legítimo (user pagante de alto LTV):**
> 51	  - Operator faz `INSERT INTO mcoin_transactions (user_id, action, amount, context) VALUES (<uid>, 'canvas_cap_override', 100, '{"reason":"manual_review","approved_by":"Sovereign"}')`.
> 52	  - Como `amount > 0`, NÃO conta no `spent_today` (que soma `abs(amount)` mas filtra por `action LIKE 'canvas_%'` — override match mas é positivo, então sum acaba subtraindo).
> 53	  - **TODO Phase 5:** mudar query do cap para `WHERE amount < 0` (mais robusto). Por ora, override usa action diferente: `canvas_credit_grant` (não matchea o LIKE).
> 54	- **Cap falha-aberto (query DB erro):** código atual loga e continua. Pillar 4 (Observability) captura via `infra_health_logs`.
> 55	- **User abusa (>100/dia repetido):** rate-limit no nível conta — Phase Commercial bloqueia tentativas excessivas em janela curta.
> 56	
> 57	## Success signal
> 58	
> 59	- `canvas-execute` retorna 402 quando `spent_today + required > cap`.
> 60	- `mcoin_transactions` tem TODA spend canvas com `action LIKE 'canvas_%'`.
> 61	- `DashboardLayout` mostra balance pill amber quando `mco_balance < 50`.
> 62	- Toast renderiza quando user cruza threshold.
> 63	
> 64	## Anti-patterns
> 65	
> 66	- ❌ Aumentar cap silenciosamente (>100) para resolver complaint específico — usar override row, manter cap visível.
> 67	- ❌ Cobrar antes do cap check — vira sangria de moedas em failed gens.
> 68	- ❌ Cap por janela calendário (00:00–23:59) — abre brecha para gastar 100 às 23:59 e mais 100 às 00:01 = 200 em 2 min. Janela rolante 24h é defensiva.
> 69	- ❌ Action tagging genérico (`spend` para tudo) — quebra a query do cap.
> 70	
> 71	## Referências
> 72	
> 73	- `supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts` (daily cap implementation + action tagging)
> 74	- `supabase/migrations/20260516224541_deduct_mco_coins_ledger.sql` (ledger 4-arg RPC)
> 75	- `src/components/dashboard/DashboardLayout.tsx` (toast + amber pill)
> 76	- `.claude/context/survival-audit-v1.md` §2 Pillar 3 (motivação)
> 77	- `.claude/context/survival-audit-v2.md` (re-audit pós-implementação)
> 78	
> ```

canal `canvas_jobs_<user_id>` | useCanvasJobsRealtime invalida queries + atualiza Zustand store via callback | DevTools \u2192 Network \u2192 WS \u2192 frame UPDATE com status='success'; UI swap badge queued \u2192 success sem refresh |\n\n## Verification gates (Lei 1 \u2014 Materiality)\n\nComandos reproduz\u00edveis a serem citados no `/handoff`:\n\n```bash\n# Gate 2 \u2014 row inserted\npsql \"$DATABASE_URL\" -c \"SELECT id, status, operation_id, started_at, webhook_token\n                          FROM vm_canvas_executions WHERE id = '<uuid>';\"\n\n# Gate 3 \u2014 Higgsfield in-flight\ncurl -H \"Authorization: Key $HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY:$HIGGSFIELD_API_KEY_SECRET\" \\\n     \"https://platform.higgsfield.ai/requests/<operation_id>/status\"\n\n# Gate 4-5 \u2014 webhook finalize\npsql \"$DATABASE_URL\" -c \"SELECT status, output_url, webhook_received_at, completed_at,\n                                response_payload->>'request_id' AS hf_req\n                          FROM vm_canvas_executions WHERE id = '<uuid>';\"\n\n# Gate 6 \u2014 atomic deduct\npsql \"$DATABASE_URL\" -c \"SELECT amount, action, context FROM mcoin_transactions\n                          WHERE context->>'execution_id' = '<uuid>'\n                          ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1;\"\n```\n\n## Recovery path\n\n| Falha | Detec\u00e7\u00e3o | A\u00e7\u00e3o | Resultado |\n|-------|----------|------|-----------|\n| **Webhook nunca chega** (Higgsfield down, network blip, token corrupted) | `vm_canvas_executions.status IN ('queued','running') AND created_at < now() - interval '10 min'` | Cron `*/5 * * * *` em `scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh` faz GET no Higgsfield status_url | Se completed \u2192 invoca webhook URL manualmente (idempotente via HTTP 409). Se failed \u2192 marca status='failed'. |\n| **Higgsfield retorna failed/nsfw** | Status no poll \u00e9 `failed`/`nsfw`/`cancelled` | Watchdog PATCH PostgREST `status='failed'` + `error_message` | Sem deduct (nunca aconteceu). Higgsfield auto-refunda hf credits. |\n| **Job stuck > 60min** | `started_at < now() - interval '60 min'` ainda em queued/running | Watchdog PATCH `status='timeout'` | Sem deduct. Sovereign decide manual refund se necess\u00e1rio. |\n| **Webhook chega mas upload falha** | Catch block em higgsfield-webhook | UPDATE status='failed' + error_message | Sem deduct (atomic ordering protege). Higgsfield auto-refunda. |\n| **Webhook idempot\u00eancia (retry)** | Higgsfield reenvia mesmo callback (at\u00e9 2h) | webhook query `WHERE webhook_token = ?` + check `status IN ('queued','running')` | HTTP 409 \u2192 no-op. Ledger e asset n\u00e3o duplicam. |\n| **Operator override (job \u00f3rf\u00e3o)** | Sovereign decis\u00e3o manual | `UPDATE vm_canvas_executions SET status='cancelled', error_message='<motivo>' WHERE id=<uuid>` | Sem deduct. Documentar em decision node se >5/m\u00eas (sinal de bug). |\n\n### Install do watchdog cron\n\n```bash\n# Verify script execut\u00e1vel\nchmod +x scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh\n\n# Dry-run (sem in-flight jobs):\nbash scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh\n# Esperado: \"0 jobs to check\" + insert em infra_health_logs\n\n# Install em crontab:\ncrontab -l > /tmp/cron.bak\n(crontab -l; echo \"*/5 * * * * /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh >> /var/log/mcorch-canvas-watchdog.log 2>&1\") | crontab -\n\n# Verify:\ncrontab -l | grep canvas-video-watchdog\n```\n\n## Success signal\n\n- `canvas-execute` retorna 200 `{status:'queued'}` em <2s para image_to_video.\n- `vm_canvas_executions` row com `operation_id` IS NOT NULL em <5s.\n- `webhook_received_at` populado em <2min (DoP Standard) ou <3min (Kling/Seedance quando habilitados).\n- `vm_canvas_assets` row com `file_size_bytes >= 100*1024` + `mime_type LIKE 'video/%'`.\n- `mcoin_transactions` linha negativa com `action='canvas_video_spend'`.\n- Browser: `<video>` element renderizado no `ImageToVideoNode` sem refresh manual.\n- `infra_health_logs` health pulse para `service IN ('canvas-execute','higgsfield-webhook','canvas-video-watchdog')` com `status='healthy'` no happy path.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- \u274c **Polling s\u00edncrono dentro de canvas-execute para video** \u2014 excede 90s edge function timeout, queima cr\u00e9dito sem entregar URL.\n- \u274c **Deduzir mcoCoins antes do upload OK** \u2014 quebra invariante \"atomic s\u00f3-ap\u00f3s-upload-OK\"; se download falha, user paga por nada.\n- \u274c **Webhook token reutilizado entre executions** \u2014 quebra defesa contra replay; migration garante `UNIQUE INDEX` em `webhook_token`.\n- \u274c **Skip do health pulse em failure paths** \u2014 observabilidade quebrada; SSP-01 OE03 Pillar 4 requer best-effort pulse mesmo em erro.\n- \u274c **Run-All bloqueante esperando v\u00eddeo terminar** \u2014 UX morre. Fire-and-forget: submit retorna, Realtime atualiza depois.\n- \u274c **Habilitar Lite/Turbo/Kling/Seedance sem confirmar endpoint** \u2014 gasta hf credits em 404. Manter `dop-standard-5s` \u00fanico at\u00e9 Phase 4.3.\n\n## Refer\u00eancias\n\n- `supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts` (async submit branch \u2014 Step 2)\n- `supabase/functions/higgsfield-webhook/index.ts` (j\u00e1 deployed v6.4.0 \u2014 finaliza\u00e7\u00e3o at\u00f4mica)\n- `supabase/migrations/20260516224542_vm_canvas_executions_async_video.sql` (already applied \u2014 colunas async)\n- `src/hooks/useCanvasJobsRealtime.ts` (subscribe + onUpdate callback \u2014 Step 3)\n- `src/pages/CanvasEditorPage.tsx` (wiring Realtime + isRunnable \u2014 Step 4)\n- `src/components/canvas/RightPanel/inspectors/ImageToVideoInspector.tsx` (Run button enable \u2014 Step 5)\n- `scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh` (cron defesa-em-profundidade \u2014 Step 6)\n- `.claude/context/higgsfield-api-validation-2026-05-15.md` (API spec can\u00f4nica + webhook idempotency)\n- `.claude/proposals/vm-canvas-jobs-async-video-v1.md` (proposal base de Phase 4.2b)\n- `docs/processes/canvas-daily-cap-handling.md` (cap aplicado upstream \u2014 n\u00e3o conflita com fluxo async)\n"
}

endpoint-validated\n  // (validation doc \u00a7 Endpoints can\u00f4nicos). Tier gate above enforces body.model === 'dop-standard'.\n  const BASE_URL = \"https://platform.higgsfield.ai\";\n  const modelPath = \"higgsfield-ai/dop/standard\";\n  const authHeader = `Key ${apiKey}:${apiSecret}`;\n\n  // Per-execution token guards against URL leak across rows (UNIQUE index on\n  // vm_canvas_executions.webhook_token). Higgsfield POSTs back with this token\n  // and the webhook idempotency check rejects replays via HTTP 409.\n  const webhookUrl = `${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/higgsfield-webhook?token=${execution.webhook_token}`;\n\n  const submitUrl = new URL(`${BASE_URL}/${modelPath}`);\n  submitUrl.searchParams.set(\"hf_webhook\", webhookUrl);\n\n  let submitResp: Response;\n  try {\n    submitResp = await fetch(submitUrl.toString(), {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: {\n        Authorization: authHeader,\n        \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n        Accept: \"application/json\",\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        image_url: imageUrl,\n        prompt: body.prompt,\n        duration,\n        motion_strength: motionStrength,\n      }),\n    });\n  } catch (e) {\n    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : \"Higgsfield submit network error\";\n    return fail(`Higgsfield submit network error: ${msg}`, 502);\n  }\n\n  if (!submitResp.ok) {\n    const text = await submitResp.text().catch(() => \"\");\n    let msg = `Higgsfield submit ${submitResp.status}: ${text.slice(0, 200)}`;\n    if (submitResp.status === 401 || submitResp.status === 403) {\n      msg = \"Higgsfield: credenciais inv\u00e1lidas\";\n    } else if (submitResp.status === 402) {\n      msg = \"Higgsfield: saldo de cr\u00e9ditos esgotado\";\n    } else if (submitResp.status === 429) {\n      msg = \"Higgsfield: limite de requisi\u00e7\u00f5es atingido\";\n    }\n    return fail(msg, 502);\n  }\n\n  let submitData: { request_id?: string; id?: string; status?: string };\n  try {\n    submitData = await submitResp.json();\n  } catch {\n    return fail(\"Higgsfield: resposta inv\u00e1lida (JSON parse failed)\", 502);\n  }\n\n  const operationId = submitData.request_id ?? submitData.id;\n  if (!operationId) {\n    return fail(\"Higgsfield: resposta de submit sem request_id\", 502);\n  }\n\n  const { error: updErr } = await admin\n    .from(\"vm_canvas_executions\")\n    .update({ operation_id: operationId })\n    .eq(\"id\", execution.id);\n  if (updErr) {\n    console.error(\"[canvas-execute] failed to persist operation_id:\", updErr);\n    // The job is in-flight at Higgsfield but we lost the polling handle.\n    // Webhook will still finalize via webhook_token; watchdog poll falls back to noop.\n  }\n\n  await admin\n    .from(\"infra_health_logs\")\n    .insert({ service: \"canvas-execute\", status: \"healthy\" })\n    .then(() => {}, (e) => console.error(\"[canvas-execute] health log failed:\", e));\n\n  return new Response(\n    JSON.stringify({\n      execution_id: execution.id,\n      status: \"queued\",\n      webhook_token: execution.webhook_token,\n      operation_id: operationId,\n      credits_charged: execution.credits_charged, // intent, not yet deducted\n      higgsfield_status: submitData.status ?? \"queued\",\n    }),\n    { headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } },\n  );\n}"
}
```

  "old_string": "import { useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\nimport { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';\n\n/**\n * Subscribes to vm_canvas_executions UPDATEs for the current user via Supabase Realtime.\n * Invalidates TanStack Query keys on every status transition so the UI re-fetches without polling.\n *\n * Used by Phase 4.2b async video flow: higgsfield-webhook flips status `queued \u2192 success/failed`,\n * this hook turns that into a re-render in CanvasEditorPage.\n *\n * Pattern mirrors `useMcorchMemory.ts:91-112` (canonical Realtime subscription in the repo).\n * RLS-aligned: filter `user_id=eq.<auth.uid>` ensures cross-tenant isolation at the channel level.\n */\nexport function useCanvasJobsRealtime(projectId?: string): void {\n  const qc = useQueryClient();\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!user) return;\n\n    const channel = supabase\n      .channel(`canvas_jobs_${user.id}`)\n      .on(\n        'postgres_changes',\n        {\n          event: 'UPDATE',\n          schema: 'public',\n          table: 'vm_canvas_executions',\n          filter: `user_id=eq.${user.id}`,\n        },\n        (payload) => {\n          const row = payload.new as { id: string; project_id: string; status: string };\n          qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-execution', row.id] });\n          if (projectId && row.project_id === projectId) {\n            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-assets', projectId] });\n            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-executions', projectId] });\n          }\n        },\n      )\n      .subscribe();\n\n    return () => {\n      supabase.removeChannel(channel);\n    };\n  }, [user?.id, projectId, qc]);\n}",
  "new_string": "import { useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\nimport { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';\n\n/**\n * vm_canvas_executions row shape relayed by the Realtime callback. Includes\n * the columns Phase 4.2c needs to reconcile the Zustand store on the client.\n */\nexport interface CanvasExecutionRow {\n  id: string;\n  project_id: string;\n  node_id: string | null;\n  status: 'pending' | 'queued' | 'running' | 'success' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'timeout';\n  output_url: string | null;\n  error_message: string | null;\n  completed_at: string | null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Subscribes to vm_canvas_executions UPDATEs for the current user via Supabase Realtime.\n * Invalidates TanStack Query keys on every status transition AND invokes the optional\n * `onUpdate` callback so callers can reconcile the Zustand canvas store with the row.\n *\n * Used by Phase 4.2c async video flow: higgsfield-webhook flips status\n * `queued \u2192 success/failed/timeout`, this hook (a) refetches query caches and\n * (b) delegates the node-level status sync to the caller via `onUpdate`.\n *\n * Pattern mirrors `useMcorchMemory.ts:91-112` (canonical Realtime subscription).\n * RLS-aligned: `user_id=eq.<auth.uid>` filter ensures cross-tenant isolation at\n * the channel level. The callback is the single bridge between Realtime \u2192 Zustand,\n * keeping the store as the source of truth for node visual state.\n */\nexport function useCanvasJobsRealtime(\n  projectId?: string,\n  onUpdate?: (row: CanvasExecutionRow) => void,\n): void {\n  const qc = useQueryClient();\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!user) return;\n\n    const channel = supabase\n      .channel(`canvas_jobs_${user.id}`)\n      .on(\n        'postgres_changes',\n        {\n          event: 'UPDATE',\n          schema: 'public',\n          table: 'vm_canvas_executions',\n          filter: `user_id=eq.${user.id}`,\n        },\n        (payload) => {\n          const row = payload.new as CanvasExecutionRow;\n          qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-execution', row.id] });\n          if (projectId && row.project_id === projectId) {\n            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-assets', projectId] });\n            qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-executions', projectId] });\n          }\n          onUpdate?.(row);\n        },\n      )\n      .subscribe();\n\n    return () => {\n      supabase.removeChannel(channel);\n    };\n  }, [user?.id, projectId, qc, onUpdate]);\n}"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/lib/canvas-utils.ts",
  "content": "import type { Edge } from '@xyflow/react';\nimport type { CanvasNode } from '@/types/canvas';\n\n/**\n * Read an image URL out of a node's `data` payload. Looks at the canonical\n * output shape (`data.output.imageUrl` / `imageDataUrl`) plus the legacy\n * `data.imageUrl` field used by CharacterReference uploads.\n *\n * Centralized in Phase 4.2c so ImageToVideoNode, ImageToVideoInspector,\n * and CanvasEditorPage.handleRunAll share the exact same resolution rule.\n */\nexport function pickNodeImage(data: unknown): string | undefined {\n  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return undefined;\n  const d = data as Record<string, unknown>;\n  const out = d.output as { imageUrl?: string; imageDataUrl?: string } | undefined;\n  if (out?.imageUrl) return out.imageUrl;\n  if (out?.imageDataUrl) return out.imageDataUrl;\n  if (typeof d.imageUrl === 'string') return d.imageUrl;\n  return undefined;\n}\n\n/**\n * Walk the immediate upstream nodes (edges where `target === nodeId`) and\n * return the first usable image URL. One hop only \u2014 matches the existing\n * inspector/node behavior; deep BFS is not needed for the current canvas\n * topology (image-to-video has a single image source).\n */\nexport function findUpstreamImage(\n  nodeId: string,\n  nodes: CanvasNode[],\n  edges: Edge[],\n): string | undefined {\n  const upstreamIds = edges\n    .filter((e) => e.target === nodeId)\n    .map((e) => e.source);\n  for (const id of upstreamIds) {\n    const src = nodes.find((n) => n.id === id);\n    if (!src) continue;\n    const url = pickNodeImage(src.data);\n    if (url) return url;\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n"
}
```

  "old_string": "/** Does this node kind have a runnable prompt + execute path right now? */\nfunction isRunnable(node: CanvasNode): boolean {\n  const d = node.data;\n  if (d.kind === \"characterReference\") return false; // upload-only\n  if (d.kind === \"imageToVideo\") return false; // Phase 4.2b\n  if (d.status === \"running\") return false;\n  const prompt = \"prompt\" in d ? d.prompt : \"\";\n  return Boolean(prompt && prompt.trim());\n}",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Per-kind gate WITHOUT the upstream-image check (for non-video kinds).\n * `imageToVideo` adds an upstream check via `findUpstreamImage` at the call site\n * inside `CanvasShell` so it has access to the current `edges` array.\n */\nfunction isRunnableBase(node: CanvasNode): boolean {\n  const d = node.data;\n  if (d.kind === \"characterReference\") return false; // upload-only\n  if (d.kind === \"imageToVideo\") return false;       // gated by upstream image at call site\n  if (d.status === \"running\") return false;\n  const prompt = \"prompt\" in d ? d.prompt : \"\";\n  return Boolean(prompt && prompt.trim());\n}"
}

  "old_string": "  // Run-All: BFS over runnable nodes (skips Character/ImageToVideo).\n  const runnable = useMemo(() => nodes.filter(isRunnable), [nodes]);\n  const runAllCost = useMemo(\n    () => runnable.reduce((sum, n) => sum + estimateNodeCost(n.data), 0),\n    [runnable],\n  );\n  const [runAllPending, setRunAllPending] = useState(false);\n\n  const handleRunAll = async () => {\n    if (!projectId || runnable.length === 0) return;\n    setRunAllPending(true);\n    let ok = 0;\n    for (const n of runnable) {\n      const payload = resolveExecutePayload(n.data);\n      if (!payload) continue;\n      updateNodeData(n.id, { status: \"running\", error: undefined });\n      try {\n        const result = await executeNode.mutateAsync({\n          project_id: projectId,\n          node_id: n.id,\n          ...payload,\n        });\n        updateNodeData(n.id, {\n          status: \"success\",\n          output: {\n            imageUrl: result?.output_url ?? undefined,\n            generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n          },\n        });\n        ok += 1;\n      } catch (e) {\n        const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);\n        updateNodeData(n.id, { status: \"failed\", error: msg });\n      }\n    }\n    setRunAllPending(false);\n    refetchDashboard?.();\n    toast.success(`Execu\u00e7\u00e3o conclu\u00edda \u00b7 ${ok}/${runnable.length} n\u00f3s`);\n  };",
  "new_string": "  // Run-All: BFS over runnable nodes. Phase 4.2c \u2014 imageToVideo joins the set\n  // when it has both a prompt and a resolvable upstream image. Video runs are\n  // fire-and-forget (status stays 'running' after submit; Realtime finalizes).\n  const runnable = useMemo(() => {\n    return nodes.filter((n) => {\n      if (n.data.kind === \"imageToVideo\") {\n        if (n.data.status === \"running\") return false;\n        const hasPrompt = Boolean(n.data.prompt && n.data.prompt.trim());\n        if (!hasPrompt) return false;\n        return Boolean(findUpstreamImage(n.id, nodes, edges));\n      }\n      return isRunnableBase(n);\n    });\n  }, [nodes, edges]);\n  const videoQueueCount = useMemo(\n    () => runnable.filter((n) => n.data.kind === \"imageToVideo\").length,\n    [runnable],\n  );\n  const runAllCost = useMemo(\n    () => runnable.reduce((sum, n) => sum + estimateNodeCost(n.data), 0),\n    [runnable],\n  );\n  const [runAllPending, setRunAllPending] = useState(false);\n\n  // Phase 4.2c \u2014 Realtime bridge: webhook flips vm_canvas_executions.status,\n  // we reconcile the Zustand store so the canvas node UI updates without refresh.\n  useCanvasJobsRealtime(projectId, (row) => {\n    if (!row.node_id) return;\n    if (row.status === \"success\") {\n      updateNodeData(row.node_id, {\n        status: \"success\",\n        output: {\n          videoUrl: row.output_url ?? undefined,\n          imageUrl: row.output_url ?? undefined,\n          generatedAt: row.completed_at ?? new Date().toISOString(),\n        },\n        error: undefined,\n      });\n      refetchDashboard?.();\n    } else if (\n      row.status === \"failed\" ||\n      row.status === \"timeout\" ||\n      row.status === \"cancelled\"\n    ) {\n      updateNodeData(row.node_id, {\n        status: \"failed\",\n        error: row.error_message ?? row.status,\n      });\n    } else if (row.status === \"queued\" || row.status === \"running\") {\n      updateNodeData(row.node_id, { status: \"running\" });\n    }\n  });\n\n  const handleRunAll = async () => {\n    if (!projectId || runnable.length === 0) return;\n    setRunAllPending(true);\n    let ok = 0;\n    for (const n of runnable) {\n      const payload = resolveExecutePayload(n.data);\n      if (!payload) continue;\n      const isVideo = n.data.kind === \"imageToVideo\";\n      const inputAssetUrl = isVideo ? findUpstreamImage(n.id, nodes, edges) : undefined;\n      updateNodeData(n.id, { status: \"running\", error: undefined });\n      try {\n        const result = await executeNode.mutateAsync({\n          project_id: projectId,\n          node_id: n.id,\n          ...payload,\n          ...(inputAssetUrl ? { input_asset_url: inputAssetUrl } : {}),\n        });\n        if (isVideo) {\n          // Async job submitted \u2014 leave status 'running'; Realtime finalizes.\n          ok += 1;\n        } else {\n          updateNodeData(n.id, {\n            status: \"success\",\n            output: {\n              imageUrl: result?.output_url ?? undefined,\n              generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n            },\n          });\n          ok += 1;\n        }\n      } catch (e) {\n        const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);\n        updateNodeData(n.id, { status: \"failed\", error: msg });\n      }\n    }\n    setRunAllPending(false);\n    refetchDashboard?.();\n    const queuedSuffix = videoQueueCount > 0\n      ? ` \u00b7 ${videoQueueCount} v\u00eddeo${videoQueueCount > 1 ? \"s\" : \"\"} aguardando webhook`\n      : \"\";\n    toast.success(`Execu\u00e7\u00e3o submetida \u00b7 ${ok}/${runnable.length} n\u00f3s${queuedSuffix}`);\n  };"
}

data: ImageToVideoData };\n  projectId: string;\n  creditsBalance: number;\n}\n\nconst DURATIONS: VideoDuration[] = [5, 10, 15];\nconst ARS: VideoAspectRatio[] = [\"9:16\", \"16:9\", \"1:1\"];\nconst MODELS: VideoModelId[] = [\n  \"dop-lite-5s\",\n  \"dop-turbo-5s\",\n  \"dop-standard-5s\",\n  \"kling-2.1-pro-5s\",\n  \"seedance-v1-pro-5s\",\n];\n\nfunction pickUpstreamImage(data: unknown): string | undefined {\n  if (!data || typeof data !== \"object\") return undefined;\n  const d = data as Record<string, unknown>;\n  const out = d.output as { imageUrl?: string; imageDataUrl?: string } | undefined;\n  if (out?.imageUrl) return out.imageUrl;\n  if (out?.imageDataUrl) return out.imageDataUrl;\n  if (typeof d.imageUrl === \"string\") return d.imageUrl as string;\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nexport function ImageToVideoInspector({ node, creditsBalance }: Props) {\n  const updateNodeData = useCanvasStore((s) => s.updateNodeData);\n  const edges = useCanvasStore((s) => s.edges);\n  const nodes = useCanvasStore((s) => s.nodes);\n\n  const info = VIDEO_MODEL_INFO[node.data.videoModel];\n  const cost = info.coinCost;\n\n  const sourceImageUrl = useMemo(() => {\n    const upstream = edges\n      .filter((e) => e.target === node.id)\n      .map((e) => nodes.find((n) => n.id === e.source))\n      .filter((n): n is NonNullable<typeof n> => !!n);\n    for (const u of upstream) {\n      const url = pickUpstreamImage(u.data);\n      if (url) return url;\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  }, [edges, nodes, node.id]);",
