> ```
> 1	#!/bin/bash
> 2	# MCORCH Intelligence Node Watchdog
> 3	# Monitors mcorch_claude_mem container on port 37777 and restarts if unhealthy.
> 4	# Also writes health telemetry to Supabase infra_health_logs for the Neural HUD.
> 5	
> 6	LOG_FILE="/var/log/mcorch-watchdog.log"
> 7	CONTAINER="mcorch_claude_mem"
> 8	PORT=37777
> 9	DATE=$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
> 10	COMPOSE_DIR="/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra"
> 11	
> 12	log() { echo "[$DATE] $1" >> "$LOG_FILE"; }
> 13	
> 14	# Check if container is running
> 15	if ! docker inspect --format='{{.State.Running}}' "$CONTAINER" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "true"; then
> 16	    log "ALERT: $CONTAINER not running. Starting via docker compose..."
> 17	    cd "$COMPOSE_DIR" && docker compose up -d mcorch-intelligence-node
> 18	    log "Restart attempted."
> 19	    HEALTH="unhealthy"
> 20	else
> 21	    # Ensure lock file exists (prevents false unhealthy state)
> 22	    docker exec "$CONTAINER" touch /app/system/autonomic.lock 2>/dev/null
> 23	
> 24	    # Check health status
> 25	    HEALTH=$(docker inspect --format='{{.State.Health.Status}}' "$CONTAINER" 2>/dev/null)
> 26	    if [ "$HEALTH" = "unhealthy" ]; then
> 27	        log "ALERT: $CONTAINER is unhealthy. Restarting..."
> 28	        docker restart "$CONTAINER"
> 29	        log "Container restarted."
> 30	    fi
> 31	fi
> 32	
> 33	log "Watchdog check completed. Status: ${HEALTH:-unknown}"
> 34	
> 35	# ── Write health telemetry to Supabase for Neural HUD ────────────────────────
> 36	# Load env vars (SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY)
> 37	if [ -f "${COMPOSE_DIR}/.env" ]; then
> 38	    # shellcheck disable=SC1090
> 39	    set -a; source "${COMPOSE_DIR}/.env"; set +a
> 40	fi
> 41	
> 42	if [ -z "${SUPABASE_URL}" ] || [ -z "${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" ]; then
> 43	    log "WARNING: SUPABASE_URL or SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY not set — skipping telemetry write."
> 44	    exit 0
> 45	fi
> 46	
> 47	# Check Chroma vector engine
> 48	CHROMA_STATUS="unhealthy"
> 49	if curl -sf "http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
> 50	    CHROMA_STATUS="healthy"
> 51	fi
> 52	
> 53	# Write a row per service (upsert-style: just insert, table keeps history)
> 54	write_health() {
> 55	    local SERVICE="$1"
> 56	    local STATUS="$2"
> 57	    curl -s -X POST "${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/infra_health_logs" \
> 58	        -H "apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
> 59	        -H "Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
> 60	        -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
> 61	        -d "{\"service\":\"${SERVICE}\",\"status\":\"${STATUS}\"}" > /dev/null 2>&1
> 62	}
> 63	
> 64	write_health "mcorch_claude_mem" "${HEALTH:-unknown}"
> 65	write_health "mcorch_chroma"     "${CHROMA_STATUS}"
> 66	
> 67	log "Telemetry written: claude_mem=${HEALTH:-unknown}, chroma=${CHROMA_STATUS}"
> 68	
> 69	# ── Layer 2 escalation: OpenClaw engineer agent ──────────────────────────────
> 70	# Layer 1 (above) tries restart; if status is still unhealthy for 3 consecutive
> 71	# cycles (≈15 min), escalate to the OpenClaw engineer agent. The agent reads
> 72	# its inbox at /tmp/openclaw-incidents/ (world-writable, ephemeral) and may
> 73	# also receive a best-effort POST to the gateway. Counter resets on healthy.
> 74	FAIL_COUNTER_FILE="/tmp/mcorch-watchdog-fail-counter"
> 75	ESCALATION_THRESHOLD=3
> 76	
> 77	# Compute a single unhealthy signal from the two monitored services.
> 78	UNHEALTHY="false"
> 79	if [ "${HEALTH:-unknown}" = "unhealthy" ] || [ "${HEALTH:-unknown}" = "unknown" ]; then
> 80	    UNHEALTHY="true"
> 81	fi
> 82	if [ "${CHROMA_STATUS}" = "unhealthy" ]; then
> 83	    UNHEALTHY="true"
> 84	fi
> 85	
> 86	if [ "${UNHEALTHY}" = "true" ]; then
> 87	    COUNT=$(cat "${FAIL_COUNTER_FILE}" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
> 88	    COUNT=$((COUNT + 1))
> 89	    echo "${COUNT}" > "${FAIL_COUNTER_FILE}"
> 90	    log "Layer 2 fail counter: ${COUNT}/${ESCALATION_THRESHOLD}"
> 91

> 97	        INCIDENT_FILE="${INCIDENT_DIR}/${INCIDENT_ID}.json"
> 98	        cat > "${INCIDENT_FILE}" 2>/dev/null <<INCIDENT_EOF
> 99	{

> 101	  "container": "${CONTAINER}",
> 102	  "claude_mem_status": "${HEALTH:-unknown}",
> 103	  "chroma_status": "${CHROMA_STATUS}",
> 104	  "fail_count": ${COUNT},

> 108	}
> 109	INCIDENT_EOF
> 110	        log "ESCALATION → engineer agent inbox: ${INCIDENT_FILE}"
> 111	
> 112	        # Best-effort gateway notification (endpoint discovery is engineer's job)
> 113	        if curl -sf "http://127.0.0.1:18789/health" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
> 114	            curl -sf -X POST "http://127.0.0.1:18789/agents/engineer/incidents" \
> 115	                -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
> 116	                --data "@${INCIDENT_FILE}" > /dev/null 2>&1 \
> 117	                && log "Gateway POST accepted." \
> 118	                || log "Gateway POST not accepted (endpoint may not exist — file inbox is durable)."
> 119	        else
> 120	            log "Gateway unreachable — escalation is file-only this cycle."
> 121	        fi
> 122	
> 123	        write_health "openclaw-escalation" "triggered:${CONTAINER}:${COUNT}"
> 124	    fi
> 125	else
> 126	    # Healthy: reset counter
> 127	    if [ -f "${FAIL_COUNTER_FILE}" ]; then
> 128	        rm -f "${FAIL_COUNTER_FILE}"
> 129	        log "Layer 2 fail counter reset (healthy state)."
> 130	    fi
> 131	fi

 log \"exec=${EXEC_ID} hf=completed \u2192 webhook redeliver HTTP ${REDELIVER_CODE}\"\n            RECOVERED_COUNT=$((RECOVERED_COUNT + 1))\n            ;;\n        failed|nsfw|cancelled)\n {
```json
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/lib/canvas-execute-result.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Shared helper to validate canvas-execute responses. The Edge Function\n * returns HTTP 200 even when the underlying provider fails (it logs the\n * error into `vm_canvas_executions.error_message` and returns a structured\n * body with `status: 'failed'` + `error: <msg>`). Inspectors must not\n * blindly treat the resolved mutation as success \u2014 they MUST inspect the\n * payload and re-throw on backend-reported failure.\n *\n * Root cause memory: smoke test 2026-05-17 \u2014 OpenRouter image endpoint\n * returned 404 HTML, backend correctly set status='failed' with\n * credits_charged=0, but inspector toast lied \"Gera\u00e7\u00e3o conclu\u00edda\".\n */\n\nexport interface CanvasExecuteResult {\n  execution_id?: string;\n  status?: string;\n  output_url?: string | null;\n  asset_id?: string | null;\n  credits_charged?: number;\n  credits_remaining?: number;\n  webhook_token?: string;\n  operation_id?: string;\n  error?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Returns the result unchanged on success. Throws on backend-reported failure\n * so callers can use a single try/catch path. The thrown Error carries the\n * backend's pt-BR message verbatim \u2014 safe to surface via toast.\n */\nexport function assertExecuteSuccess<T extends CanvasExecuteResult | undefined | null>(\n  result: T,\n): T {\n  if (!result) {\n    throw new Error(\"canvas-execute retornou resposta vazia\");\n  }\n  if (result.error) {\n    throw new Error(result.error);\n  }\n  if (result.status === \"failed\" || result.status === \"timeout\" || result.status === \"cancelled\") {\n    throw new Error(`Gera\u00e7\u00e3o ${result.status}`);\n  }\n  return result;\n}\n"
}
```

> 41	export function SceneComposeInspector({
> 42	  node,
> 43	  projectId,

> 47	  const updateNodeData = useCanvasStore((s) => s.updateNodeData);

> 80	          generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
> 81	        },

> 83	      });

> 39	export function StyleTransferInspector({

> 62	      .filter((e) => e.target === node.id)
> 63	      .map((e) => nodes.find((n) => n.id === e.source))
> 64	      .filter((n): n is NonNullable<typeof n> => !!n);
> 65	    for (const u of upstream) {

> 68	    }
> 69	    return undefined;
> 70	  }, [edges, nodes, node.id]);

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/migrations/20260518012541_canvas_assets_update_policy.sql",
  "content": "-- Phase 4.2c hotfix \u2014 canvas-assets UPDATE policy\n-- Root cause: CharacterReferenceInspector calls supabase.storage.upload() with\n-- `upsert: true`, which Supabase storage-api translates to PG-side\n-- `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`. The ON CONFLICT path requires both INSERT\n-- AND UPDATE policies \u2014 but migration 20260514040200 only defined INSERT/SELECT/DELETE.\n-- Result: HTTP 400 \"new row violates row-level security policy\" on every\n-- character reference upload (reproduced 2026-05-17 smoke test).\n--\n-- Fix: add USING + WITH CHECK UPDATE policy mirroring the INSERT policy shape.\n-- Source: docs/processes/canvas-video-async-execution.md \u00a7 Recovery + Sovereign report.\n\nCREATE POLICY \"Users update own canvas assets\" ON storage.objects\n  FOR UPDATE TO authenticated\n  USING (\n    bucket_id = 'canvas-assets'\n    AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::TEXT\n  )\n  WITH CHECK (\n    bucket_id = 'canvas-assets'\n    AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::TEXT\n  );\n"
}
```

Both `gpt-image-1` (new model) and `dall-e-3` reject this parameter when passed to OpenAI `/v1/images/generations` endpoint. Quality/style params (lines 381–382) ARE model-conditional but response_format is not. Line 392 attempts to parse `data.data[0].b64_json`, but OpenAI returns `url` field instead when b64_json rejected. **Fix:** Detect model; omit `response_format` for models that don\'t support it OR switch to `url` response format.\n\n---\n\n## Bug 2 — OpenRouter "image generation" sumiu (404 HTML)\n\n**File:** `/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts:447–474` (generateOpenRouter)\n\n**Root cause:** Line 450 calls deprecated endpoint `https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/images/generations` (returns 404 Next.js HTML). OpenRouter sunset this path; image generation now routes via `/v1/chat/completions` with `modalities: ["image", "text"]`. MODEL_INFO entry (types/canvas.ts:176–183) maps `openrouter-dall-e-3` → modelParam `"dall-e-3"` but backend doesn\'t switch to chat-completions path. UI masks this (GenerateImageInspector.tsx:62–66 hides OpenRouter group; deprecation note: "endpoint returns 404 HTML as of 2026-05-17"). **Fix:** Replace fetch in generateOpenRouter with chat-completions call, set `modalities`, parse image_url from response.content[0].image_url.url.\n\n---\n\n## Bug 3 — Replicate "API key not configured"\n\n**File:** No input field exists in SettingsPage.tsx for replicate_api_key (lines 110–165 populate keys state with groq/openrouter/openai/elevenlabs/google/nanobana/seedance but NOT replicate).\n\n**Root cause:** Backend (canvas-execute:102) reads `userKeys?.replicate_api_key || Deno.env.get("REPLICATE_API_KEY")`. SettingsPage never saves replicate_api_key to user_api_keys table, so userKeys.replicate_api_key is always undefined. User falls back to Deno env var (empty in Sovereign\'s request). Hook `useUserApiKeys` (hooks/useUserApiKeys.ts:11–28) queries user_api_keys table; any field missing from SettingsPage form won\'t surface. **Fix:** Add KeyField component for Replicate API key in SettingsPage (after line 346 openai_api_key) + include in keys state (line 128).\n\n---\n\n## Bug 4 — Storage RLS "new row violates row-level security policy" — FIXED in migration but potentially not deployed remotely\n\n**File:** Migration order:\n- 20260514040200_viralmind_canvas_studio.sql lines 139–144: INSERT, SELECT, DELETE policies only\n- 20260518012541_canvas_assets_update_policy.sql lines 12–21: UPDATE policy added (root cause: upsert=true requires UPDATE)\n\n**Root cause:** CharacterReferenceInspector.tsx line 45 calls `upload(path, file, { upsert: true })`. Supabase storage translates upsert → INSERT...ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE. Migration 20260514040200 only defined INSERT/SELECT/DELETE; no UPDATE policy. Migration 20260518012541 fixes by adding UPDATE policy (lines 12–21, same USING/WITH CHECK shape as INSERT). Client uses publishable key (VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY, client.ts line 6). **Critical:** Migration was added 2026-05-18 but smoke test failure was 2026-05-17 — migration likely not applied to remote DB yet. Run `supabase migration list` on production to confirm 20260518012541 status.'}]
> ```

> 
> CREATE POLICY "canvas_assets_update"
>   ON storage.objects FOR UPDATE TO public
>   USING (
>     bucket_id = 'canvas-assets'
>     AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL
>     AND ((storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text OR owner = auth.uid())
>   )
>   WITH CHECK (
>     bucket_id = 'canvas-assets'
>     AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL
>     AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text
>   );
> 
> CREATE POLICY "canvas_assets_delete"
>   ON storage.objects FOR DELETE TO public
>   USING (
>     bucket_id = 'canvas-assets'
>     AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL
>     AND ((storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text OR owner = auth.uid())
>   );
> 
> CREATE POLICY "canvas_assets_select"
>   ON storage.objects FOR SELECT TO public
>   USING (bucket_id = 'canvas-assets');
> ```
> 
> Pós-migration, verificar via aios-sql-bridge (admin JWT) ou Supabase dashboard que as 4 policies existem com a forma esperada.

> bun run test          # 122/122
> bun run build         # CanvasEditorPage chunk ~115 kB

> #       - openrouter-gemini-image → image gera (paid via OR credits)
> #       - flux-schnell → image gera (paid via Replicate)

```json
  "new_string": "async function generateOpenAI(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {\n  if (!apiKey) throw new Error(\"OpenAI API key not configured\");\n\n  const model = body.model || \"dall-e-3\";\n  const size = body.parameters?.size || \"1024x1024\";\n\n  // Round 3 hotfix (2026-05-17): OpenAI rejected `response_format` for both\n  // `gpt-image-1` AND `dall-e-3` on the /v1/images/generations endpoint with\n  // HTTP 400 \"Unknown parameter\". We now omit the param \u2014 OpenAI defaults to\n  // returning a URL (for dall-e-*) or b64_json (for gpt-image-1). The caller\n  // upload block already handles both shapes via {url, b64} return contract.\n  const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {\n    model,\n    prompt: body.prompt,\n    n: 1,\n    size,\n  };\n  // quality + style only apply to dall-e-3; gpt-image-1 ignores them.\n  if (body.parameters?.quality) payload.quality = body.parameters.quality;\n  if (body.parameters?.style) payload.style = body.parameters.style;\n\n  const resp = await fetch(\"https://api.openai.com/v1/images/generations\", {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n    body: JSON.stringify(payload),\n  });\n\n  if (!resp.ok) {\n    const t = await resp.text();\n    throw new Error(`OpenAI error ${resp.status}: ${t}`);\n  }\n\n  const data = await resp.json();\n  // gpt-image-1 returns b64_json by default; dall-e-* returns url. Caller's\n  // upload block prefers b64 (direct upload) but falls back to URL download.\n  return { url: data.data?.[0]?.url || \"\", b64: data.data?.[0]?.b64_json || \"\" };\n}"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts",
  "old_string": "    // \u2500\u2500 Upload to storage if we have b64 \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    let storedUrl = outputUrl;\n    if (outputB64 && !error) {\n      try {\n        const bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(outputB64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));\n        const fileName = `${user.id}/${execution.id}.png`;\n        const { error: uploadErr } = await admin.storage\n          .from(\"canvas-assets\")\n          .upload(fileName, bytes, { contentType: \"image/png\" });\n        if (!uploadErr) {\n          const { data: urlData } = admin.storage\n            .from(\"canvas-assets\")\n            .getPublicUrl(fileName);\n          storedUrl = urlData.publicUrl;\n        }\n      } catch (uploadErr) {\n        console.error(\"Upload error:\", uploadErr);\n      }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    // \u2500\u2500 Upload to storage (b64 OR URL response) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    // Round 3 hotfix: OpenAI defaults to URL response (no response_format).\n    // OpenRouter chat-completions multimodal returns URL. Both paths must\n    // persist to canvas-assets so the provider URL's short TTL doesn't break\n    // the asset_url stored in vm_canvas_assets. Pattern mirrors rescue-video\n    // (Google Veo) + higgsfield-webhook (DoP video) download+upload flow.\n    let storedUrl = outputUrl;\n    if (!error && (outputB64 || outputUrl)) {\n      try {\n        let bytes: Uint8Array | null = null;\n        let contentType = \"image/png\";\n\n        if (outputB64) {\n          bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(outputB64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));\n        } else if (outputUrl) {\n          // Fetch the provider's signed URL, validate, then re-host in bucket.\n          const dlResp = await fetch(outputUrl);\n          if (dlResp.ok) {\n            const ct = dlResp.headers.get(\"content-type\") || \"\";\n            if (ct.startsWith(\"image/\")) contentType = ct;\n            bytes = new Uint8Array(await dlResp.arrayBuffer());\n          } else {\n            console.error(\"[canvas-execute] provider URL fetch failed:\", dlResp.status);\n          }\n        }\n\n        if (bytes && bytes.byteLength > 0) {\n          const ext = contentType.split(\"/\")[1]?.split(\";\")[0] || \"png\";\n          const fileName = `${user.id}/${execution.id}.${ext}`;\n          const { error: uploadErr } = await admin.storage\n            .from(\"canvas-assets\")\n            .upload(fileName, bytes, { contentType, upsert: true });\n          if (!uploadErr) {\n            const { data: urlData } = admin.storage\n              .from(\"canvas-assets\")\n              .getPublicUrl(fileName);\n            storedUrl = urlData.publicUrl;\n          } else {\n            console.error(\"[canvas-execute] upload error:\", uploadErr);\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (uploadErr) {\n        console.error(\"Upload error:\", uploadErr);\n      }\n    }"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "    // \u2500\u2500 Upload to storage (b64 OR URL response) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    // Round 3 hotfix: OpenAI defaults to URL response (no response_format).\n    // OpenRouter chat-completions multimodal returns URL. Both paths must\n    // persist to canvas-assets so the provider URL's short TTL doesn't break\n    // the asset_url stored in vm_canvas_assets. Pattern mirrors rescue-video\n    // (Google Veo) + higgsfield-webhook (DoP video) download+upload flow.\n    let storedUrl = outputUrl;\n    if (!error && (outputB64 || outputUrl)) {\n      try {\n        let bytes: Uint8Array | null = null;\n        let contentType = \"image/png\";\n\n        if (outputB64) {\n          bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(outputB64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));\n        } else if (outputUrl) {\n          // Fetch the provider's signed URL, validate, then re-host in bucket.\n          const dlResp = await fetch(outputUrl);\n          if (dlResp.ok) {\n            const ct = dlResp.headers.get(\"content-type\") || \"\";\n            if (ct.startsWith(\"image/\")) contentType = ct;\n            bytes = new Uint8Array(await dlResp.arrayBuffer());\n          } else {\n            console.error(\"[canvas-execute] provider URL fetch failed:\", dlResp.status);\n          }\n        }\n\n        if (bytes && bytes.byteLength > 0) {\n          const ext = contentType.split(\"/\")[1]?.split(\";\")[0] || \"png\";\n          const fileName = `${user.id}/${execution.id}.${ext}`;\n          const { error: uploadErr } = await admin.storage\n            .from(\"canvas-assets\")\n            .upload(fileName, bytes, { contentType, upsert: true });\n          if (!uploadErr) {\n            const { data: urlData } = admin.storage\n              .from(\"canvas-assets\")\n              .getPublicUrl(fileName);\n            storedUrl = urlData.publicUrl;\n          } else {\n            console.error(\"[canvas-execute] upload error:\", uploadErr);\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (uploadErr) {\n        console.error(\"Upload error:\", uploadErr);\n      }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    // \u2500\u2500 Upload to storage (b64 OR URL response) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    // Round 3 hotfix: OpenAI defaults to URL response (no response_format).\n    // OpenRouter chat-completions multimodal returns URL. Both paths must\n    // persist to canvas-assets so the provider URL's short TTL doesn't break\n    // the asset_url stored in vm_canvas_assets. Pattern mirrors rescue-video\n    // (Google Veo) + higgsfield-webhook (DoP video) download+upload flow.\n    let storedUrl = outputUrl;\n    let storedKey: string | null = null;\n    let storedContentType = \"image/png\";\n    if (!error && (outputB64 || outputUrl)) {\n      try {\n        let bytes: Uint8Array | null = null;\n        let contentType = \"image/png\";\n\n        if (outputB64) {\n          bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(outputB64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));\n        } else if (outputUrl) {\n          // Fetch the provider's signed URL, validate, then re-host in bucket.\n          const dlResp = await fetch(outputUrl);\n          if (dlResp.ok) {\n            const ct = dlResp.headers.get(\"content-type\") || \"\";\n            if (ct.startsWith(\"image/\")) contentType = ct;\n            bytes = new Uint8Array(await dlResp.arrayBuffer());\n          } else {\n            console.error(\"[canvas-execute] provider URL fetch failed:\", dlResp.status);\n          }\n        }\n\n        if (bytes && bytes.byteLength > 0) {\n          const ext = contentType.split(\"/\")[1]?.split(\";\")[0] || \"png\";\n          const fileName = `${user.id}/${execution.id}.${ext}`;\n          const { error: uploadErr } = await admin.storage\n            .from(\"canvas-assets\")\n            .upload(fileName, bytes, { contentType, upsert: true });\n          if (!uploadErr) {\n            const { data: urlData } = admin.storage\n              .from(\"canvas-assets\")\n              .getPublicUrl(fileName);\n            storedUrl = urlData.publicUrl;\n            storedKey = fileName;\n            storedContentType = contentType;\n          } else {\n            console.error(\"[canvas-execute] upload error:\", uploadErr);\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (uploadErr) {\n        console.error(\"Upload error:\", uploadErr);\n      }\n    }"
}
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts",
  "old_string": "async function generateOpenRouter(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {\n  if (!apiKey) throw new Error(\"OpenRouter API key not configured\");\n\n  const resp = await fetch(\"https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/images/generations\", {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: {\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n      \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n      \"HTTP-Referer\": \"https://mcorch.com\",\n      \"X-Title\": \"GCRUX Canvas Studio\",\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      model: body.model || \"dall-e-3\",\n      prompt: body.prompt,\n      n: 1,\n      size: body.parameters?.size || \"1024x1024\",\n      response_format: \"b64_json\",\n    }),\n  });\n\n  if (!resp.ok) {\n    const t = await resp.text();\n    throw new Error(`OpenRouter error ${resp.status}: ${t}`);\n  }\n\n  const data = await resp.json();\n  return { url: data.data?.[0]?.url || \"\", b64: data.data?.[0]?.b64_json || \"\" };\n}",
  "new_string": "async function generateOpenRouter(apiKey: string, body: ExecuteRequest) {\n  if (!apiKey) throw new Error(\"OpenRouter API key not configured\");\n\n  // Round 3 hotfix (2026-05-17): /v1/images/generations returns 404 HTML on\n  // OpenRouter \u2014 endpoint was deprecated. OpenRouter image generation now\n  // routes through /v1/chat/completions with `modalities: [\"image\", \"text\"]`\n  // for models that support image OUTPUT (e.g. google/gemini-2.5-flash-image-preview,\n  // black-forest-labs/flux-1.1-pro). Response shape: choices[0].message.content\n  // is an array of multimodal parts; image parts have type `image_url` with\n  // `image_url.url` carrying the asset URL.\n  const model = body.model || \"google/gemini-2.5-flash-image-preview\";\n\n  const resp = await fetch(\"https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions\", {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: {\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n      \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n      \"HTTP-Referer\": \"https://mcorch.com\",\n      \"X-Title\": \"GCRUX Canvas Studio\",\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      model,\n      modalities: [\"image\", \"text\"],\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: body.prompt }],\n    }),\n  });\n\n  if (!resp.ok) {\n    const t = await resp.text();\n    throw new Error(`OpenRouter error ${resp.status}: ${t.slice(0, 200)}`);\n  }\n\n  const data = await resp.json();\n  const message = data?.choices?.[0]?.message;\n  if (!message) throw new Error(\"OpenRouter: resposta sem message\");\n\n  // Two output shapes seen in the wild:\n  //   1. message.content = string (text only \u2014 image missing)\n  //   2. message.content = [{type:'text',text:...}, {type:'image_url', image_url:{url:'data:...' | 'https://...'}}]\n  //   3. message.images = [{url:'...'}] (some OR providers return separate field)\n  let outUrl = \"\";\n  let outB64 = \"\";\n\n  if (Array.isArray(message.content)) {\n    for (const part of message.content) {\n      if (part?.type === \"image_url\" && part?.image_url?.url) {\n        const u = String(part.image_url.url);\n        if (u.startsWith(\"data:image/\")) {\n          outB64 = u.split(\",\", 2)[1] ?? \"\";\n        } else {\n          outUrl = u;\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!outUrl && !outB64 && Array.isArray(data?.choices?.[0]?.message?.images)) {\n    const first = data.choices[0].message.images[0];\n    if (first?.url) {\n      const u = String(first.url);\n      if (u.startsWith(\"data:image/\")) outB64 = u.split(\",\", 2)[1] ?? \"\";\n      else outUrl = u;\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!outUrl && !outB64) {\n    throw new Error(\"OpenRouter: resposta multimodal sem image_url (modelo pode n\u00e3o suportar sa\u00edda de imagem)\");\n  }\n\n  return { url: outUrl, b64: outB64 };\n}"
}
```

> 1	import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
> 2	import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
> 3	import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
> 4	import type { Database } from '@/integrations/supabase/types';
> 5	
> 6	export type UserApiKeysRow = Database['public']['Tables']['user_api_keys']['Row'];
> 7	type UserApiKeysUpdate = Database['public']['Tables']['user_api_keys']['Update'];
> 8	
> 9	const QUERY_KEY = (userId: string | undefined) => ['user_api_keys', userId] as const;
> 10	
> 11	export function useUserApiKeys() {
> 12	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 13	  const qc = useQueryClient();
> 14	
> 15	  const { data: userKeys, isLoading } = useQuery({
> 16	    queryKey: QUERY_KEY(user?.id),
> 17	    queryFn: async () => {
> 18	      const { data, error } = await supabase
> 19	        .from('user_api_keys')
> 20	        .select('*')
> 21	        .eq('user_id', user!.id)
> 22	        .maybeSingle();
> 23	      if (error) throw error;
> 24	      return data as UserApiKeysRow | null;
> 25	    },
> 26	    enabled: !!user?.id,
> 27	    staleTime: 60_000,
> 28	  });
> 29	
> 30	  const { mutateAsync: saveKeys, isPending: isSaving } = useMutation({
> 31	    mutationFn: async (payload: Partial<UserApiKeysUpdate>) => {
> 32	      const { error } = await supabase
> 33	        .from('user_api_keys')
> 34	        .upsert({
> 35	          ...payload,
> 36	          user_id: user!.id,
> 37	          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
> 38	        } as UserApiKeysUpdate);
> 39	      if (error) throw error;
> 40	    },
> 41	    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: QUERY_KEY(user?.id) }),
> 42	  });
> 43	
> 44	  return { userKeys, saveKeys, isSaving, isLoading };
> 45	}
> 46

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/migrations/20260518020030_canvas_assets_rls_repair.sql",
  "content": "-- Phase 4.2c Round 3 \u2014 canvas-assets storage RLS REPAIR\n-- Root cause: smoke test 2026-05-17 still hits 403 \"new row violates row-level\n-- security policy\" on character upload despite migration 20260514040200:139-144\n-- defining the policies in the expected shape. Two suspected causes:\n--   (a) policies in 20260514040200 may not have applied to remote DB (only the\n--       INSERT INTO storage.buckets succeeded; CREATE POLICY statements skipped\n--       or errored silently);\n--   (b) `TO authenticated` is not evaluating with the new sb_publishable_* anon\n--       key format used by login.mcorch.com \u2014 switching to `TO public` with an\n--       explicit `auth.uid() IS NOT NULL` guard is defensive against either.\n--\n-- Defense in depth: also accept `owner = auth.uid()` for UPDATE/DELETE so\n-- existing rows owned by the user (storage-api auto-populates owner column on\n-- INSERT) work regardless of folder-prefix path conventions.\n--\n-- DROP IF EXISTS makes this migration idempotent \u2014 safe to re-run.\n\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Users upload own canvas assets\" ON storage.objects;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Users update own canvas assets\" ON storage.objects;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Users delete own canvas assets\" ON storage.objects;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"Public read canvas assets\" ON storage.objects;\n-- Also drop the names from migration 20260518012541 (UPDATE-only repair) so we\n-- consolidate into a single canonical set.\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"canvas_assets_insert\" ON storage.objects;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"canvas_assets_update\" ON storage.objects;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"canvas_assets_delete\" ON storage.objects;\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS \"canvas_assets_select\" ON storage.objects;\n\n-- INSERT \u2014 user can upload to their own folder. TO public + auth.uid() check\n-- works under both sb_publishable_* (new) and eyJ... (legacy) anon keys.\nCREATE POLICY \"canvas_assets_insert\"\n  ON storage.objects FOR INSERT TO public\n  WITH CHECK (\n    bucket_id = 'canvas-assets'\n    AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL\n    AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text\n  );\n\n-- UPDATE \u2014 needed for upload({upsert: true}) which translates to INSERT\n-- ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE under the hood.\nCREATE POLICY \"canvas_assets_update\"\n  ON storage.objects FOR UPDATE TO public\n  USING (\n    bucket_id = 'canvas-assets'\n    AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL\n    AND (\n      (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text\n      OR owner = auth.uid()\n    )\n  )\n  WITH CHECK (\n    bucket_id = 'canvas-assets'\n    AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL\n    AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text\n  );\n\n-- DELETE \u2014 user can remove their own assets.\nCREATE POLICY \"canvas_assets_delete\"\n  ON storage.objects FOR DELETE TO public\n  USING (\n    bucket_id = 'canvas-assets'\n    AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL\n    AND (\n      (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text\n      OR owner = auth.uid()\n    )\n  );\n\n-- SELECT \u2014 bucket is public; anyone can read. Mirrors prior policy.\nCREATE POLICY \"canvas_assets_select\"\n  ON storage.objects FOR SELECT TO public\n  USING (bucket_id = 'canvas-assets');\n"
}
```

## PHASE 1 — SECURITY AUDIT

Run these checks and report findings. **Block the seal if any CRITICAL finding exists.**

> ⚠️ **SCOPE NOTE:** This phase scans changes that exist NOW (before Phase 5 writes HANDOFF.md).
> A second mandatory scan runs in Phase 5b, after HANDOFF.md is written and before it is committed.

```bash
# 1a. Secret leak scan — check staged + working tree for hardcoded credentials
git diff HEAD | grep -E "(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{20,}|STRIPE_|SECRET_KEY|ACCESS_TOKEN|api_key\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]{10,})" \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-key\|YOUR_" | head -20

# 1b. Edge function JWT enforcement — every user-facing function must verify JWT
grep -rL "Authorization\|jwt\|JWT\|service_role" supabase/functions/*/index.ts \
  | grep -v "get-infra-status\|watchdog" || echo "ALL FUNCTIONS: JWT enforced ✅"

# 1c. Client-side coin deduction guard — must NEVER update mco_balance directly from client
grep -rn "mco_balance.*update\|UPDATE.*mco_balance" src/ \
  | grep -v "settings\|top.up\|topup\|SettingsPage" | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"

# 1d. RLS bypass risk — check for supabase.rpc calls without auth context
grep -rn "service_role\|bypass.*rls\|rls.*bypass" src/ | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"

# 1e. Sensitive data in console.log
grep -rn "console\.log.*token\|console\.log.*key\|console\.log.*secret\|console\.log.*password" src/ \
  | head -10 || echo "NO SENSITIVE LOGS ✅"
```

**Security verdict:** list each check as ✅ PASS / ⚠️ WARN / 🔴 BLOCK.
Only proceed if no 🔴 findings.

---

### DOCUMENTATION CREDENTIAL RULE (invariant — never violate)

**When writing HANDOFF.md, README.md, or any committed documentation:**

- ✅ DO: describe WHERE a credential is stored → `VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY` is set in `.env` (local) and `GEMINI_API_KEY` in the Supabase vault
- ✅ DO: reference digest/fingerprint if useful → `GEMINI_API_KEY (digest 0fe0e159...)`
- 🔴 NEVER: include the actual credential value → `AIzaSyBv...`, `sk-...`, `eyJ...`

> **Rationale:** Phase 1 scans changes that predate the HANDOFF.md write. Any credential embedded
> in HANDOFF.md during Phase 5 escapes Phase 1 entirely and goes directly to the remote.
> This rule is the primary prevention layer; Phase 5b is the detection backstop.

---

## PHASE 2 — GRANULAR COMMITS

Group uncommitted changes by concern and commit each group separately.

**Commit rules (from memory + project convention):**
- Prefix: `feat:`, `fix:`, `refactor:`, `chore:`, `docs:`
- Body: one paragraph explaining the WHY (milestone orientation, not diff description)
- **NEVER** add `Co-Authored-By` trailer
- **NEVER** use `git add -A` — stage files explicitly by name
- Skip: `build_log.txt`, `node_modules/`, `dist/`, `.env`, `check_types.ts`, `scratch/`

Stage and commit each logical group before moving to the next phase.

```bash
SESSION_PHASE="<phase-name-slug>"
SESSION_SUMMARY="<one-sentence summary of what was accomplished>"

curl -s -X POST "${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes" \
  -H "apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Prefer: return=representation" \
  -d "{
    \"name\": \"session-handoff-$(date +%Y%m%d)-${SESSION_PHASE}\",
    \"node_type\": \"handoff\",
    \"content\": \"[HANDOFF SEAL] ${SESSION_SUMMARY}\",
    \"stability_score\": 1.0,
    \"project_id\": null,
    \"user_id\": null,
    \"metadata\": {
      \"session\": \"${SESSION_PHASE}\",
      \"sealed_at\": \"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\",
      \"commits\": \"$(git log --oneline -5 | head -5 | tr '\n' '|')\"
    }
  }" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print('Node ID:', d[0]['id'] if isinstance(d,list) else d.get('id','error'))" 2>/dev/null \
  || echo "⚠️ Knowledge Mesh insert failed — log manually"
```

Then trigger embedding for the new node:
```bash
NODE_ID="<id-from-above>"
curl -s -X POST "${SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/embed-mcorch-node" \
  -H "apikey: ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer ${SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY}" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d "{\"record\": {\"id\": \"${NODE_ID}\"}}" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print('Embedded:', d)" 2>/dev/null \
  || echo "⚠️ Embedding failed — node persisted but not vectorized"
```

## PHASE 5b — HANDOFF.md SECRET SCAN (mandatory before commit)

**Run this scan on the HANDOFF.md content about to be committed. Block if any finding exists.**

```bash
# 5b-1. Scan HANDOFF.md for real credential values
grep -nE "(AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}|sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{40,}|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[a-zA-Z0-9]{32,}=\s*['\"]?[A-Za-z0-9+/]{40,})" HANDOFF.md \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-key\|YOUR_\|digest\|sha256\|hash\|fingerprint" \
  | head -20 || echo "HANDOFF.md: NO CREDENTIALS FOUND ✅"

# 5b-2. Specifically check for Google API keys (AIza prefix = 39 chars total)
grep -n "AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]\{35\}" HANDOFF.md | head -10 || echo "NO GOOGLE API KEYS ✅"

# 5b-3. Check for any = "value" pattern that looks like a real assignment
grep -nE "=\s*['\"][A-Za-z0-9_\-]{20,}['\"]" HANDOFF.md \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-\|YOUR_\|digest\|hash\|uuid\|id.*[0-9a-f-]\{36\}" \
  | head -10 || echo "NO INLINE ASSIGNMENTS ✅"
```

**If any check returns a match:**
1. 🔴 **DO NOT COMMIT** HANDOFF.md
2. Rewrite the offending section — replace the actual value with its location description
3. Re-run Phase 5b until all checks pass
4. Only then proceed to commit HANDOFF.md

Commit HANDOFF.md only after Phase 5b passes:
```
docs(handoff): seal <phase name> — <one-line summary>
```

---

## PHASE 6 — BoK SEAL STATUS (mandatory before push)

**Verify any significant module work in this session has a sealed BoK suite per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.**

```bash
# 6-1. List BoK suites present + check completeness
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] \
    && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete (9 docs)" \
    || echo "⚠️ $slug — incomplete (missing: ${missing[*]})"
done

# 6-2. Check session touched code in new src/pages/ or new supabase/functions/ that lacks BoK
session_new_modules=$(git diff --name-only HEAD~10..HEAD 2>/dev/null | grep -E "^(src/pages/|supabase/functions/|src/components/[A-Z])" | awk -F/ '{print "/"}' | sort -u)
echo "Session touched modules: $session_new_modules"

# 6-3. Mesh seal nodes for sealed BoK suites
source .env 2>/dev/null
if [ -n "$SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" ]; then
  curl -s "$SUPABASE_URL/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?node_type=eq.documentation_suite&select=id,name,stability_score,created_at" \
    -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
    | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); [print(f\"  🌐 {n['name']} ({n['stability_score']})\") for n in d]" 2>/dev/null \
    || echo "  ⚠️ Could not query seal nodes"
fi
```

**Gate verdict:**
- ✅ All session modules have sealed BoK → proceed to Phase 7 push.
- ⚠️ Module work without BoK (e.g. `feat:` commit creating new page/edge fn without `docs/bok/<slug>/`)
  → 🔴 **BLOCK seal**. Run `/bok-scribe <module>` first OR add module to exemption list (typo/dep bump/single-file patch).

Report BoK status in final seal output (Phase 8).

Migration `20260518020030_canvas_assets_rls_repair.sql` (idempotente) faz DROP IF EXISTS de 8 nomes poss\u00edveis + recria 4 policies (`canvas_assets_insert/update/delete/select`) com `TO public + auth.uid() IS NOT NULL` (defensivo contra publishable key `sb_publishable_*` que pode n\u00e3o resolver `TO authenticated` corretamente) + `owner = auth.uid()` fallback em UPDATE/DELETE. **Material proofs:** TS zero erros \u00b7 122/122 testes em 4.40s \u00b7 build em 23.55s \u00b7 `CanvasEditorPage` chunk 112.61 \u2192 113.66 kB (gzip 33.03 kB) \u00b7 `canvas-execute` script size 132.6 \u2192 **138.3 kB** (+5.7 kB) \u00b7 2 commits granulares (`6554cb4` canvas src 16 files +976/-110 \u00b7 `76c99e3` 3 migrations +104) \u00b7 3 migrations aplicadas via `supabase db push` \u00b7 milestone node `eb0b3ae0-9ec1-4328-ad31-1b079d3f294e` embedado (768 dims openai/text-embedding-3-small, 1 chunk) \u00b7 saldo Sovereign **5533** intacto (zero cobran\u00e7a em qualquer execu\u00e7\u00e3o failed) \u00b7 mesh total **8336 nodes** (+8 desde v6.6.1, inclui handoff seal). **Pr\u00f3ximo deliverable:** (a) smoke test pago Sovereign \u2014 DoP Standard 5s = $0.56 \u2014 valida flywheel end-to-end (gates 4-9 da SOP); (b) cron install do watchdog (`crontab -e` adicionando linha `*/5 * * * * scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh`) ap\u00f3s smoke test passar; (c) OTD-CV42C-001 dynamic OR model picker \u2014 spec separada Phase 4.3; (d) Pipeline node chaining (SceneCompose/StyleTransfer ainda n\u00e3o recebem upstream image \u2014 discuss\u00e3o tem como gate Higgsfield Soul Character Phase 4.5).\n\n**PANTHEON CONFIGURATION + MEMORY WIKI SEED v6.6.1 SHIPPED (2026-05-17).**"

```json
  "new_string": "**Sprint v6.6.2 selado em 2026-05-17** \u2014 Canvas Studio Phase 4.2c \u2014 Async Video Pipeline Live: tr\u00eas rounds em sess\u00e3o \u00fanica destravando todos os providers de imagem do Canvas + backbone async Higgsfield. **(Round 1)** Phase 4.2c wiring shipped \u2014 `canvas-execute` ganha branch dedicada `image_to_video` que submete ao endpoint `higgsfield-ai/dop/standard` com `?hf_webhook=<URL-encoded callback>` (per validation doc), persiste `vm_canvas_executions.operation_id` + `webhook_token` (64-hex unique), retorna `{status:'queued'}` em <2s; d\u00e9bito at\u00f4mico s\u00f3 no webhook ap\u00f3s upload-OK (`deduct_mco_coins` `action='canvas_video_spend'`). Hook `useCanvasJobsRealtime` extendido com callback `onUpdate(row)` que reconcilia Zustand store. `CanvasEditorPage` monta Realtime + Run-All BFS fire-and-forget para video. `ImageToVideoInspector` Run button real + tier-gate UI (apenas `dop-standard-5s` clic\u00e1vel; Lite/Turbo/Kling/Seedance disabled com tooltip \"Aguardando valida\u00e7\u00e3o tier \u2014 Phase 4.3\"). SOP `docs/processes/canvas-video-async-execution.md` (~190 LOC) escrita ANTES do c\u00f3digo (Lei 2). Watchdog cron `scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh` (~95 LOC, bash + PostgREST + jq) \u2014 poll Higgsfield a cada 5min, marca `timeout` em jobs travados >60min, idempotente via HTTP 409 do webhook. Dry-run validado material: `[2026-05-17 21:54:35] 0 jobs to check` + health UUID `38c860f5-8d8d-4ae7-b54d-316f26678410` em `infra_health_logs`. **(Round 2 \u2014 assertExecuteSuccess Lei 1)** Smoke test revelou inspector mentindo: toast `\"Gera\u00e7\u00e3o conclu\u00edda \u2014 15 \ud83d\udc8e debitados\"` quando backend marcava `status='failed'` (saldo Sovereign confirmou intacto em 5533 \u2014 frontend mentindo, backend correto). Causa raiz: `canvas-execute` retorna HTTP 200 + `error` field, `mutateAsync` n\u00e3o throw, frontend tratava qualquer 200 como sucesso. Novo util `src/lib/canvas-execute-result.ts` faz `assertExecuteSuccess<T>(result)` que THROW em `result.error` set OU `status \u2208 {failed,timeout,cancelled}`. Aplicado em 4 inspectors + Run-All. Toast agora usa `result.credits_charged ?? cost` (zero quando falha). Migration `20260518012541_canvas_assets_update_policy.sql` adiciona UPDATE policy (necess\u00e1ria para `upload({upsert:true})` que vira `INSERT...ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`). **(Round 3 \u2014 4 bugs novos)** **(A)** OpenAI `dall-e-3` E `gpt-image-1` HTTP 400 `Unknown parameter: 'response_format'`. Removido o param fixo; upload block agora baixa URL provider\u2192canvas-assets server-side (mirror `rescue-video:39-77` + `higgsfield-webhook:144-167`); `storedKey`/`storedContentType` propagados pro asset row. **(B)** OpenRouter `/v1/images/generations` retorna 404 HTML (endpoint deprecated). `generateOpenRouter` migrado para `/v1/chat/completions` com `modalities:[\"image\",\"text\"]` parseando `choices[0].message.content[].image_url.url` (+ fallback `message.images[]`). Cat\u00e1logo `types/canvas.ts` ganha 2 entries OR funcionais (`openrouter-gemini-image` $0.039 + `openrouter-flux-pro` $0.04); `openrouter-dall-e-3` permanece deprecated com banner amber. `MODEL_GROUPS` restaurado. OTD-CV42C-001 registrada para Phase 4.3 (picker din\u00e2mico via OR `/v1/models` com free tier). **(C)** Replicate \"key not configured\" \u2014 coluna `replicate_api_key` NUNCA existia em `user_api_keys`. Migration `20260518020028_user_api_keys_replicate.sql` adiciona. `types.ts` ganha campo manual em Row/Insert/Update (evita full regen). `SettingsPage` ganha KeyField. Inspector ganha banner per-provider (openai/openrouter/replicate) quando user key falta com link `/dashboard/settings`. **(D)** Storage RLS 403 persistente em character upload. Investiga\u00e7\u00e3o revelou NOTICE cr\u00edtico no `db push`: `policy \"Public read canvas assets\" does not exist, skipping` \u2014 confirma que migration original `20260514040200` aplicou apenas 3 de 4 policies no remoto. Migration `20260518020030_canvas_assets_rls_repair.sql` (idempotente, DROP IF EXISTS 8 nomes + recria 4 policies) usa `TO public + auth.uid() IS NOT NULL` (defensivo contra publishable key `sb_publishable_*` que pode n\u00e3o resolver `TO authenticated`) + `owner = auth.uid()` fallback em UPDATE/DELETE. **Material proofs:** TS zero erros \u00b7 122/122 testes em 4.40s \u00b7 build em 23.55s \u00b7 `CanvasEditorPage` chunk 113.66 kB \u00b7 `canvas-execute` 138.3 kB \u00b7 4 commits granulares (`6554cb4` canvas src \u00b7 `76c99e3` 3 migrations \u00b7 `9615c69` readme \u00b7 `0d7bc07` handoff) \u00b7 push `cc828a0..0d7bc07` para origin/main \u00b7 milestone node `eb0b3ae0-9ec1-4328-ad31-1b079d3f294e` embedado (768 dims) \u00b7 saldo Sovereign **5533** intacto \u00b7 mesh total **8336 nodes** (+8 desde v6.6.1). **Pr\u00f3ximo deliverable:** (a) smoke test pago DoP Standard 5s ($0.56) \u2014 Sovereign valida gates 4-9 da SOP; (b) cron install do watchdog (`crontab -e` adicionando `*/5 * * * * scripts/canvas-video-watchdog.sh`) ap\u00f3s smoke passar; (c) OTD-CV42C-001 dynamic OR model picker \u2014 Phase 4.3 spec dedicada.\n\n**Sprint v6.6.1 selado em 2026-05-17**"
}
```
